# SADEED AI — Phase 01 Audit & Phase 02 Benchmark

2026-10-12 · audited at `ea9a947` (`origin/main` and published on Lovable at audit time; production-check run 471 passed) · read-only · 233 requirements across 7 areas · 47 P0/P1 claims adversarially verified (44 held, 3 corrected)

`origin/main` is now followed by docs-only commits: `d34db30` (docs/audit/* and a `.prettierignore` entry) and this report's commit. Application code is identical to `ea9a947`.

---

## 0. Executive summary

- **Verdict: not ready against the Phase 14 Definition of Done.** Of the owner's 12 Definition-of-Done behaviours, 0 are met, 5 are partially met and 7 are not met. After verification the 233 rows hold **1 P0, 20 P1, 116 P2, 54 P3 and 42 with no defect**; 34 rows are BROKEN, 101 PARTIAL, 42 MISSING, 12 UNVERIFIED and 44 IMPLEMENTED.
- **Release blocker — SEC-01 (P0, verified).** Anonymous visitors can receive maintenance-order creator employee/ID numbers. `src/lib/exec/maintenance-report.real.server.ts` writes `أنشأه: <created_by>` into `notes` from the git-tracked `src/lib/exec/maintenance-report.data.json` (249 rows, all with `created_by`, 20 distinct identifiers, tracked since 2026-09-02). Those notes reach the guest chat context (`chat.ts` guest branch → `buildRecallBlock` → `formatWorkOrderRecord`) and the `sec.functions` `getWorkOrders` fallback, outside the masking view `maint_orders_v` (SEC-01, SEC-25). Two contract tests currently lock this behaviour, so the fix must update them. **Next step:** execution starts at **WS-0 (this hotfix)** after explicit owner approval of §9.1 and §9.2; production DB changes (§9.3) gate WS-4, WS-5, WS-7, WS-8 and WS-10.
- **Uploaded knowledge cannot reach any answer** (RAG-01, INGEST-34, CHAT-14; all P1. The verifier downgraded RAG-01 and INGEST-34 from P0 because retrieval is flag-gated and fails closed; CHAT-14 was P1 and held.) In production `aria_match_documents` was called and failed with `42P01 relation "public.aria_document_chunks" does not exist`; the `aria_match_*` bodies still read tables renamed to `finn_*`. The error is swallowed to `console.warn`, `rag_query_log` has 0 rows, and the 8 ready documents / 18 embedded chunks are inert. Library search fails on every query (RAG-03, UX-38, SEC-15).
- **The UI says the opposite** (INGEST-35, UX-37; P1). `/knowledge-upload` prints "Citation Ready: Yes" and "SADEED can now cite this document", advertises an unenforced "up to 50MB", and shows "no documents" when the list query fails (UX-42). The composer promises to deposit every attached file, but PDFs and images are never deposited (UX-17).
- **Every chat turn is fully buffered** (CHAT-02, P1). `generateText` runs to completion, the safe-output gateway rewrites the full text, and the reply is emitted as one `text-delta`; even the HTTP headers wait for the whole turn. Before the model call an authenticated turn makes 7 strictly sequential network round trips (8 with RAG on, 9 with the HCIS read); a guest turn makes 3 (QA-21). The owner-approved "sentence by sentence behind a flag" streaming does not exist.
- **A hung turn has no upper bound** (CHAT-10, P1, verified). The client's 30 s timer is never attached because AI SDK 6 always passes `init.signal` and `sadeedAuthedFetch` prefers it; pre-model RPCs and `generateText` have no deadline.
- **The output gateway misreports and corrupts** (CHAT-17, INGEST-37, RAG-11, CHAT-05; all P1). Actions that really executed are relabelled "requires approval" (2 of 3 phrasings measured), with no idempotency guard against the repeat this invites. `RE_PHONE` rewrites ISO dates, decimal series and long reference numbers to `[هاتف محجوب]` (all 3 probe values in one table: an ISO date, a decimal series and a 10-digit reference). Because `RE_PHONE` runs on the default answer path (`finalize.ts:464`), this affects every model answer. An invented citation tag passes when it sits beside one real tag; a correct bare `SEC-02` citation is refused.
- **Chat-to-PDF export is structurally sound but loses meaning** (UX-22, P1, reproduced). The sanitizer deletes `−`, `≥`, `≤`, arrows, ✓/✗ and €, so `−3%` prints as `3%`. Long titles overflow onto the brand mark (UX-24); tables lose columns (UX-28); there is no sources section (UX-21). Entry points, palette, Arabic shaping, page numbers and size bounds are implemented (UX-20, UX-23, UX-26, UX-27, UX-34).
- **Light theme breaks the chat panel; keyboard users cannot leave it cleanly** (UI-21 P1, UI-22, UI-12, UI-13). Hard-coded dark surfaces with theme-token text give a 1.04:1 title in light mode (dark mode: 16.83:1). Escape does not close the panel (8/8 runs) and Tab focus escapes behind the scrim.
- **Arabic-first breaks at the edges.** Server and voice errors reach users as English sentences or snake_case codes (CHAT-07, UX-05); on-screen bidi misorders Latin-led paragraphs and ISO dates (UI-04); `/knowledge-upload` is English-first and shows internal endpoint names (UX-45, UI-25).
- **Nothing production-side is measured, and today's telemetry cannot measure it.** TTFT, per-stage and end-to-end latency, retrieval latency, recall@5, citation precision, indexing time, throughput and error/timeout rates are **not measured** (QA-20). `first_token_ms` is a constant `null`, `latency_ms` omits every pre-model stage, `rag_query_log` is empty, and this sandbox cannot reach the production domains.
- **Phase 02 benchmark:** on 13 dimensions SADEED is **Below reference** on 9 and **Not comparable / not verified** on 4; it **Exceeds** on none, because no comparative measurement exists (§8). These are per-dimension comparisons of documented capability, not whole-product comparisons.

### Definition of Done (Phase 14) — today

The 12 items are the owner's Phase 14 Definition of Done, quoted verbatim. Each is decided by the matrix rows listed with it (final status and severity after verification). Verdict rule, applied mechanically to every row: **Not met** = the basis holds a BROKEN or MISSING row at P0/P1, or holds no IMPLEMENTED row; **Partially met** = at least one IMPLEMENTED row and no BROKEN or MISSING row at P0/P1; **Met** = every basis row IMPLEMENTED. (An earlier draft counted an item as met when it had no BROKEN and no P0/P1 row; that let item 12 pass with no production benchmark at all, QA-20 MISSING, so the rule was tightened.)

## 1. Method & limits

### 1.1 Scope and vocabulary

Seven areas were audited at HEAD `ea9a947`: CHAT (critical path, streaming, routing, context), INGEST (knowledge ingestion and per-format support), RAG (retrieval, grounding, citations, prompt injection), UX (composer: mic, upload, PDF export; knowledge library), UI (responsive, RTL, accessibility, both themes), SEC (security, privacy, resilience) and QA (tests, observability, baselines). No application code was modified, and the local probe runs left `git status` clean. The audit's only repository writes are the interim commit `d34db30` (docs/audit/* and `.prettierignore`) and this report's commit, which also narrows that `.prettierignore` entry from the whole `docs/audit/` folder to the two generated audit file patterns.

Status vocabulary: **BROKEN** (exists but produces a wrong or failing result), **PARTIAL**, **MISSING**, **UNVERIFIED** (cannot be settled without a measurement that was not possible here), **IMPLEMENTED**. Severity: **P0** release-blocking; **P1** correctness, integrity or truthfulness defect on a core path; **P2** significant gap; **P3** minor or polish; **none** no defect found. Article 27 applies throughout: anything not measured is written as **not measured**, never as zero.

### 1.2 How evidence was gathered

- **Code tracing** with file:line evidence for every row (the Evidence column in §3).
- **Local probes, all outside the repo** (session scratch suite, not committed):
  - Vite SSR loader and esbuild bundles of `finalize.ts`, `chat-pdf.ts`, `knowledge-ingest-core.ts`, a verbatim copy of the legacy chunker, `instant-data-answers.server.ts` and `response-contract.ts`.
  - A supabase-js builder probe (postgrest-js 2.108.2) showing the rpc builder has no `.catch`.
  - fontkit glyph coverage of `public/fonts/nexios-arabic.ttf`; `pdftotext`, `pdffonts` and `pdftoppm` on generated PDFs.
  - A shape-only scan of `maintenance-report.data.json` (values not printed).
  - Targeted vitest runs (chat 6 files/33 tests; ingestion 4/52; RAG + output gateway 8/141; security 3/19; all passed).
  - Playwright on Chromium 1194 against a local `wrangler dev` preview of a scratch copy of `.output`. Only the scratch copy's `compatibility_date` was patched (to 2026-08-06, the local workerd maximum). Every non-127.0.0.1 request was aborted and `/api/chat` was mocked with an AI SDK v6 UI-message SSE stream, so the browser made no production calls.
- **Adversarial verification.** An independent verifier re-read the cited lines for all 47 P0/P1 claims and reproduced the finalize, `.catch`, PDF-sanitizer and maintenance-JSON probes. Its corrections are final in this report, and each re-checked row's full verifier reason, including its correction sentences, is reproduced at the start of that row's Evidence cell in the §3 matrix. Example (RAG-01): `finn_knowledge` HCIS rows (source `'hcis/%'`) do reach answers through a direct read (`chat.ts:566-617`); only the uploaded chunks and the other `finn_knowledge` rows cannot; and `knowledge-upload.functions.ts:171` is a stats SELECT, not a write.

### 1.3 Production facts (lead, read-only SQL via Lovable, 2026-10-12)

Treated as ground truth. Production schema cannot be inferred from `supabase/migrations/**` (CLAUDE.md lesson of 2026-09-20).

- `finn_documents`: 8 rows, all `ready`, 0 official; `UNIQUE (source, revision_hash) WHERE is_official`.
- `finn_document_chunks`: 18 rows, `vector(768)`; `ivfflat (embedding vector_cosine_ops) WITH lists=100` over 18 rows (pgvector default `ivfflat.probes` = 1); an index on `(document_id, chunk_index)` whose uniqueness is not stated; no lexical (FTS or trigram) index although `pg_trgm` is installed; pgvector 0.8.0.
- `aria_match_documents` was **called and failed**: `42P01 relation "public.aria_document_chunks" does not exist`. `aria_match_knowledge` reads the absent `aria_knowledge` (`finn_knowledge` exists, 46 rows).
- `rag_gaps` does not exist, while `rag_record_gap_for_org` (SECURITY DEFINER, authenticated EXECUTE) inserts into it.
- `rag_query_log`: 0 rows.
- RLS is enabled on all `finn_*`, `aria_*` and `rag_query_log`; `finn_documents` and `finn_document_chunks` have owner policies plus `tenant_isolation` ALL for authenticated.
- `sec_kb_hybrid_search` exists over `sec_knowledge_articles` (SECURITY INVOKER) with **anon** EXECUTE in production; the repo grants only authenticated and service_role.
- Storage buckets `uploads`, `aria-documents`, `aria-attachments`, `aria-attachments-quarantine`: private, `file_size_limit` NULL, `allowed_mime_types` NULL.
- SEC-01 was independently re-checked by the lead (see §0).

Lead facts from outside SQL (2026-10-12):

- The production database belongs to Lovable project `6f092c67-70c5-400b-9a7e-8c068475864f` (read from Lovable `get_project`).
- Lovable `query_database` accepted about 8 calls per hour (rate observed by the lead during this audit).

### 1.5 Benchmark research

13 dimensions were researched (§8). Anthropic pages (`platform.claude.com`, `support.claude.com`) were fetched directly. `help.openai.com`, `platform.openai.com`, `support.google.com` and `ai.google.dev` could not be fetched from this environment (DNS failure or proxy CONNECT 403), so the OpenAI and Google cells come from search-engine summaries of the official pages, regional mirrors, or labelled third-party sources, and carry lower confidence.

### 1.6 What could not be measured here, and why

- **Live performance:** TTFT, end-to-end and per-stage latency (guest and authenticated), Gemini latency and token counts, voice transcription latency and accuracy, TTS time to first audio, retrieval latency, recall@5, citation precision, indexing time, throughput, error and timeout rates. Reasons: the sandbox network policy blocks the production domains; provider keys are not available; `first_token_ms` is always null; `latency_ms` excludes pre-model stages; `rag_query_log` is empty.
- **Devices:** iOS Safari keyboard, voice capture and blob download. WebKit is not installed; no device was available (UI-02, UI-33, UX-09, UX-32).
- **Production environment values:** `SADEED_GEMINI_RAG_READY` (publicly readable as `rag_ready` from `GET /api/chat/health`, not read from here) and `TRUST_PROXY_HEADERS`.
- **Production schema details outside the lead's facts:** uniqueness of `(document_id, chunk_index)` (INGEST-25), FK cascade on chunks (SEC-16), `sec_knowledge_articles.embedding` dimension (RAG-07), storage policies (SEC-17), FORCE/RESTRICTIVE RLS (SEC-11), and whether migrations `20260920140000` and `20260805120000` are applied.
- **Platform behaviour:** Host-header handling (INGEST-22), `request.signal` on client disconnect (CHAT-03), survival of unawaited promises after the response (CHAT-15).

## 2. Critical paths as traced

Legend: `[IMPLEMENTED]` `[PARTIAL]` `[BROKEN]` `[MISSING]` `[UNVERIFIED]` per step; **⛔ BREAK** marks the exact point where the path stops producing the intended result. Line numbers are at HEAD `ea9a947`.

### 2.1 Chat: USER MESSAGE → CHAT API → AUTHORIZATION → CONTEXT RETRIEVAL → MODEL PROVIDER → STREAMING RESPONSE

Authenticated text turn; guest differences are noted where they diverge.

**USER MESSAGE**

1. `SadeedCopilot.tsx:1288-1328 submit()` calls `chat.sendMessage` directly, bypassing `sadeedBrain`. `[PARTIAL]` Typed turns default to `replyMode: 'voice'`: `shouldSpeakSadeedReply` returns `muted !== true` (`reply-modality.ts:16-27`), the default is unmuted (`voice-mute.ts:28`), and `submit()` sends `'voice'` (`SadeedCopilot.tsx:1307`). This applies the voice-only contract (no Markdown or tables, 1–3 sentences, 700 tokens) to typed executive reports (CHAT-23).
2. `sadeed-provider.tsx:51-52 useChat({ id: 'sadeed-singleton' })`. The id fails the server's UUID check (`-chat-guard.ts:209-215`), so `threadId` is dropped and thread persistence never runs. `[MISSING]` CHAT-13. **⛔ BREAK (continuity):** a reload loses the conversation.
3. `sadeed-service.ts:99-128 createSadeedTransport` with `windowChatMessages(20)`. `[IMPLEMENTED]` CHAT-11, QA-23 (measured: an 8 MiB attachment history shrinks from 11,190,000 to 5,060 bytes).
4. `sadeed-service.ts:35-67 sadeedAuthedFetch`: Bearer from the Supabase session; on 401/403 the same request is re-sent without Authorization. `[IMPLEMENTED]` CHAT-01. `[BROKEN]` The 30 s client timer is never attached: `signal: init.signal ?? controller.signal` (`:46, :59`) while AI SDK 6 always passes `init.signal` (`node_modules/ai/dist/index.mjs:14208, 14619`). CHAT-10. **⛔ BREAK (no upper bound on a turn).**

**CHAT API**

5. `POST /api/chat` → `chat.ts:252`; kill switch `:254`; `validateChatRequest` (`-chat-guard.ts`): content type, 24 MB Content-Length, 1–24 messages, 200k text chars, at most 5 files of 10 MiB each and 20 MiB total, MIME allowlist, UUID `threadId`, tool parts dropped. `[IMPLEMENTED]` CHAT-26.
   - `[BROKEN]` Error bodies are English sentences or snake_case codes (`rate_limit_unavailable`, `daily_identity_cap`, `ai_disabled`); the client shows them verbatim and never reads `message_ar` (CHAT-07).
   - `[MISSING]` No magic-byte inspection of file parts; the guard trusts the declared media type and data-URL prefix (INGEST-18, UX-16).
   - `[PARTIAL]` A body without Content-Length is read unbounded; guests can make the server parse up to 24 MiB of JSON before rate limiting (SEC-23).

**AUTHORIZATION**

6. `chat.ts:331 auth.getUser` → `:345 org_members` (org from `app_metadata.current_org`, never from the body; RAG fails closed on ambiguity). `[IMPLEMENTED]` SEC-03. `[BROKEN]` No deadline (CHAT-10, P1). `[PARTIAL]` Sequential (CHAT-21); a transient GoTrue or network failure also returns 401 and silently downgrades a valid user to the guest path (CHAT-01 note).
7. `chat.ts:380 check_rate_limit` → `:390 enforceAiQuota`, which makes two more sequential RPCs (`ai-quota.server.ts:182, 204`). `[PARTIAL]` Fails closed with 503 even for no-model answers (CHAT-09). Guests share the key `chat:ip:shared` (12 requests per 300 s; 120 per day) unless `TRUST_PROXY_HEADERS=true`, whose production value was not measured (CHAT-22 P1, SEC-06, QA-22).
8. Pre-model short-circuits `chat.ts:416-468`: scope guard → `matchFastRecall` → `matchInstantDataAnswer` → a fixed single-delta stream. `[PARTIAL]` The "under 400 ms" promise still waits on 3 (guest) or 5 (authenticated) round trips with no timeout (CHAT-20). `[BROKEN]` File parts are ignored: a count question about an attached log gets the system dataset count (INGEST-38; 3 of 3 bare count questions hijacked).

**CONTEXT RETRIEVAL**

9. Authenticated: `chat.ts:493-505 Promise.all[ buildSadeedContextSnapshot (15 queries, 2.5 s race) ‖ gatherRagContext ]`. Inside `rag.server.ts:82-213`: gate on `SADEED_GEMINI_RAG_READY === 'true'` and an orgId (`:96`) → 768-d query embedding (15 s timeout × 3 attempts) → `aria_match_memory`, `aria_match_knowledge`, `aria_match_documents` (`:102-106`) → production `42P01` → `console.warn` (`:108-110`) → empty context; no `rag_query_log` row. `[BROKEN]` CHAT-14, RAG-01, RAG-21. **⛔ BREAK (grounding).**
10. Guest: `chat.ts:522-541 buildProductionDatasetsSnapshot(question, now, 'anonymous')` → `sadeed-context.server.ts:69 buildRecallBlock` → `projects-maintenance-recall.server.ts:215 formatWorkOrderRecord`, which prints `notes` verbatim (notes are also a match field, `:167`). Notes carry `أنشأه: <created_by>` from `maintenance-report.real.server.ts:107`. `[BROKEN P0]` SEC-01. **⛔ BREAK (privacy, release-blocking).**
11. HCIS reference: `chat.ts:566-617` reads `finn_knowledge` where `source LIKE 'hcis/%'`, with no deadline. `[PARTIAL]` RAG-30. This direct read is the only knowledge that reaches answers today (verifier note on RAG-01); guests may get no rows.
12. Prompt assembly.
    - Retrieved text is injected at `chat.ts:520`; the `:544-552` branch is dead code (RAG-02, corrected to P3).
    - `[PARTIAL]` Excerpts are not passed through `safeExcerpt`/`sanitizeRetrievedContent` or PII redaction, and `retrievedDocs` is never given to the gateway (CHAT-16, RAG-10, SEC-08).
    - Deep council `chat.ts:650` (50 s) is unreachable from the UI (CHAT-27).
    - 31 tools, including writers, are built at `:695` (CHAT-24); writers have no idempotency (CHAT-05, P1).
    - Adaptive preferences `:725`, sequential (CHAT-21).
    - The runtime clock block is prepended at offset 0 of a 42,600-char static prompt (`:747-763`), which defeats prefix reuse (CHAT-19).
    - `startAt` is set at `:717`, after everything above, so `latency_ms` under-reports (QA-14).

**MODEL PROVIDER**

13. `chat.ts:795-810 generateText`: Gemini (3.6 flash), `stepCountIs(12)`, `maxRetries: 2` on the same model, `abortSignal: request.signal` only. `[MISSING]` No fallback model or cooldown, although CLAUDE.md layer 2 describes one and `SadeedCopilot.tsx:1859-1861` claims it (CHAT-08). `[BROKEN]` No turn deadline (CHAT-10). `[MISSING]` No streaming (CHAT-02, P1). **⛔ BREAK (perceived latency):** nothing reaches the client until this step, finalize and seal finish.
14. `chat.ts:839-848 finalizeSadeedOutput`, called with no execution receipts, no `retrievedDocs`, and `evidence.available = ragCitations` only (`:843`).
    - `[BROKEN]` Executed actions are relabelled "requires approval" (2 of 3 phrasings; CHAT-17).
    - `[BROKEN]` `RE_PHONE` (`finalize.ts:174`) rewrites `2026-10-12`, `12.5 13.7 14.2` and `4500012345` to `[هاتف محجوب]` (3 of 3; INGEST-37).
    - `[PARTIAL]` An invented tag passes beside a real one; a bare `SEC-02` with KB evidence is refused; tool citations are not counted as evidence (RAG-11).
    - **⛔ BREAK (truthfulness).**

**STREAMING RESPONSE**

15. `sealEnvelope` → `chat.ts:965-984`: one `text-start`, one `text-delta` (the whole safe text), one `text-end`, then message-metadata with `citations`, `ragCounts` and `coverage`; the `Response` is built at `:985` with `X-SADEED-Reply-Source: safe-gateway`. `[MISSING]` Streaming (CHAT-02): time to first byte equals the whole turn.
16. Persistence `chat.ts:821-959` uses fire-and-forget promises with no `waitUntil` (CHAT-15, UNVERIFIED). Guest turns write no interaction or incident rows (`if (userId)` at `:821`, `:861`). `first_token_ms` is always null (`:718`, `:890`; QA-15). The thread block `:902-945` never runs (CHAT-13).
17. Client render. `[MISSING]` Only `metadata.envelope` is read, for speech (`SadeedCopilot.tsx:1089`); citations are never rendered (RAG-12, UI-07). `[IMPLEMENTED]` react-markdown + remark-gfm, no `MessageResponse`. `[BROKEN]` On-screen bidi misorders Latin-led paragraphs, ISO dates and codes (UI-04). The error bubble masks auth wording (`:2229-2231`) but shows codes (CHAT-07). `[PARTIAL]` Auto-retry (`:1865-1885`) retries non-retryable 4xx/429 and never retries provider failures, which come back as 200 (CHAT-04). `[MISSING]` No live region announces replies or failures (UI-11).

**Break summary (2.1):** continuity (step 2), no upper bound (steps 4 and 13), grounding (step 9), privacy (step 10, P0), perceived latency (step 13), truthfulness (step 14).

### 2.2 Upload → grounded answer: UPLOAD → VALIDATION → STORAGE → EXTRACTION → CHUNKING → INDEXING → SEARCH → GROUNDED ANSWER

Canonical entry point `/knowledge-upload`. In the code, extraction runs **in the browser before storage**, and the server receives only the extracted text, never the file bytes. The stages follow actual execution order (extraction runs in the browser before storage), which differs from the requested order; step numbers follow the same order.

**UPLOAD**

1. `knowledge-upload.tsx` picker or drag-and-drop; one file at a time. The picker's accept list (`:301`) excludes `.html` and `.csv`, which drag-and-drop still accepts (INGEST-08, INGEST-09). `[BROKEN]` The page is English-first, has no `dir="rtl"` on its root, and shows internal endpoint names (UX-45, UI-25); `[PARTIAL]` INGEST-45. `[PARTIAL]` No multi-file queue (UX-35).

**VALIDATION**

2. File validation is client-side only (the type branch in `extractText`, `:60-97`). The 250,000-character text cap is checked in the browser (`:153`) and again by the server function (`knowledge-upload.functions.ts:19`) and the hook (`sadeed-docs-ingest.ts:60`). `[PARTIAL]` No byte cap, although the page advertises "up to 50MB" (`:295`); bucket `file_size_limit` and `allowed_mime_types` are NULL (INGEST-19, SEC-13). `[MISSING]` No magic-byte check: `inspectSadeedAttachment` (`src/lib/security/file-inspection.server.ts:248`) is imported only by its own test (INGEST-18; QA-07 `[BROKEN]`: the tests protect nothing).

**EXTRACTION (runs before storage)**

3. In the browser: pdf.js text (`:65-84`; items joined by spaces, pages by blank lines, no page metadata), mammoth raw text (`:85-95`; tables, headings and lists flattened), `File.text()` (`:62-63`; UTF-8 only). `[PARTIAL]` INGEST-01, INGEST-03, INGEST-05. `[MISSING]` Scanned PDFs throw "No extractable text" (`:151`; INGEST-02); PPTX has no branch (INGEST-13).

**STORAGE**

4. The original goes to private `aria-documents/${uid}/…` (`:162-171`). `[PARTIAL]` An upload error is ignored (`:170`); the title is the unsliced file name (`:176`) while the server allows 300 characters, so a longer name leaves an object with no row; originals are never downloadable (INGEST-20).
5. `knowledge-upload.functions.ts:27-163 enqueueKnowledgeIngest` inserts a `finn_documents` row as `pending` (`:38-53`). `[PARTIAL]` Dedupe runs after the insert and is not atomic (`:56-111`; INGEST-24). If `CRON_HOOK_SECRET` is missing the code throws after the insert and the row stays `pending` (`:114-115`; INGEST-26). No rate limit or AI quota (INGEST-32).
6. A synchronous HTTP self-call with `x-cron-secret` to an origin derived from the incoming request URL, with no timeout (`:117-130`). `[UNVERIFIED]` INGEST-22 / `[PARTIAL]` SEC-02 (exploitability depends on platform Host handling, not measured). `[MISSING]` Not asynchronous despite the name (INGEST-21).

**CHUNKING**

7. Hook `sadeed-docs-ingest.ts`: `chunkText` 1600/200. Lossless (a 250,000-char input gives 179–249 chunks with 0 dropped; overlap inflates embedding input by about 1.18–1.21×). `[PARTIAL]` No awareness of headings, pages or tables; overlap can split words (INGEST-31).

**INDEXING**

8. One `embedContent` call per chunk at concurrency 4, 768-d (`:124-132`); `embedGeminiBatch` (100 per call) is unused (INGEST-32). Then `upsert` with `onConflict: 'document_id,chunk_index'` (`:154-161`). `[UNVERIFIED P1]` No migration creates a matching UNIQUE index; if production's index is non-unique, every hook ingest fails with `42P10` (INGEST-25). The ANN index is `ivfflat lists=100` over 18 rows with default probes 1, and there is no lexical index (`[PARTIAL]` INGEST-33; `[UNVERIFIED]` RAG-24).
9. `status = 'ready'` (`:169-180`). The page then shows "Citation Ready: Yes" and "Ingested · SADEED can now cite this document." (`knowledge-upload.tsx:346-385`), where `citation_ready` only means every chunk has an embedding (`knowledge-upload.functions.ts:194`). `[BROKEN P1]` INGEST-35, UX-37. **⛔ BREAK (Article 27 — the claim is false).**

**SEARCH**

10. Chat: `rag.server.ts:96` requires the flag and an orgId (production flag value not measured; the runbook keeps it false) → `:105 rpc('aria_match_documents', { _user_id, … })` → `42P01`. `[BROKEN P1]` RAG-01, INGEST-34. **⛔ BREAK (retrieval).** `[PARTIAL]` The repo body filters by user only, with no org predicate (RAG-08).
11. Library search: `documents.functions.ts:187-217 searchSadeedDocuments` → the same RPC (`:202`) → throws `error.message` (`:207`). `[BROKEN P1]` RAG-03, UX-38, SEC-15. Enterprise search matches only title and the first 500 characters of the summary by ILIKE (INGEST-34 evidence).

**GROUNDED ANSWER**

12. If retrieval worked, chunks would become `[DOC:title]` citations with no page locator (`rag.server.ts:153-162`; INGEST-27) and would feed `evidence.available` (`chat.ts:843`), satisfying the regulatory evidence gate for any upload, verified or not (INGEST-42; Article 25 owner item, §9.5). Citations are not rendered (RAG-12). **Today no ingested chunk reaches any answer: the 8 ready documents and 18 chunks are inert.**

**Side paths**

- **Legacy `/sadeed-documents` → `ingestSadeedDocument`** (still linked from `marketplace.tsx:51,59` and `enterprise-search.functions.ts:164`): 2,000,000-char cap, a 900/120 chunker that silently stops at 400 chunks (measured: at least 751,322 of 2,000,000 chars dropped; max chunk 3,122 chars), null embeddings stored while the document is marked `ready`. `[BROKEN P1]` INGEST-30; chunks inserted without `org_id` `[PARTIAL]` INGEST-43. It is the only path that handles XLSX (INGEST-10).
- **Chat attachments** are sent inline as base64 to Gemini for the current turn only; later turns see metadata text only. Only text-like files are deposited, and only after the reply arrives (`SadeedCopilot.tsx:1229-1286, 1315-1316`). `[PARTIAL]` INGEST-36, UX-18.
- **Dead:** `supabase/functions/rag-ingest` targets `rag_documents`/`rag_chunks` at 1536 dims; no caller, tables absent in production (INGEST-17).

**Break summary (2.2):** false readiness (step 9), retrieval (steps 10–11); latent: chunk upsert uniqueness (step 8, unverified).

### 2.3 Chat → PDF: CHAT RESPONSE → SOURCE VALIDATION → PDF GENERATION → DOWNLOAD

**CHAT RESPONSE**

1. The server emits the safe text as one delta plus `metadata.citations` (`chat.ts:965-984`). The client keeps only `metadata.envelope` (`SadeedCopilot.tsx:1089`); citations are discarded here.
2. Entry points: whole chat (`:2407-2409`; disabled while busy or empty, `:1478-1485`) and per answer (`:2174`; `exportAnswer` `:1493-1508`). A ref blocks double clicks (`:1457`); the PDF engine is a lazy import (`:1464`); notices are Arabic. `[IMPLEMENTED]` UX-20.
3. `exportableOf` / `displayTextOf` (`:344-359`): the displayed text only, attachments by file name, no tool or reasoning parts. `[IMPLEMENTED]` UX-33, SEC-21.

**SOURCE VALIDATION**

4. None. There is no sources stage: citations were dropped at step 1, `chat-pdf.ts` has no sources section, links are flattened inline (`:84`), and the footer carries a generic disclaimer only (`:385-391`). The only citation check anywhere is the server gateway, which lets invented tags through (RAG-11). `[MISSING]` UX-21. **⛔ BREAK (traceability).**

**PDF GENERATION**

5. `chat-export.ts:19-23 toPdfMessages` keeps non-empty user/assistant text; fonts are fetched same-origin (`:57-61`). `[IMPLEMENTED]`
6. `buildChatPdf` (pdf-lib + fontkit). IBM Plex Sans Arabic is embedded (736 glyphs; full coverage of U+0621–064A, harakat, Arabic-Indic digits and Presentation Forms-B); Latin uses non-embedded Helvetica (UX-30 `[IMPLEMENTED]`). The approved palette `#38BDF8 #2563EB #F0F9FF #22D3EE #34D399 #FFFFFF #F8FAFC #0F172A` (`chat-pdf.ts:38-49`) is applied (UX-23 `[IMPLEMENTED]`).
7. `markdownToLines` (`:95-114`): table rows are flattened and empty cells dropped, so later values shift into the wrong column (UX-28 `[PARTIAL]`); code fences are parsed as Markdown and ordered items become bullets (UX-29 `[PARTIAL]`).
8. `sanitizePdfText` (`:57-64`, the SAFE_CHAR/UNDRAWABLE allow-list) deletes every character outside its allow-list: `−` (U+2212), `≥`, `≤`, arrows, ✓/✗, €. Measured: `−3%` prints as `3%`; `≥ 5 نقاط` loses its comparison. `[BROKEN P1]` UX-22. **⛔ BREAK (meaning).**
9. `layoutArabicLine` (`arabic-text.ts:154-250`, a UAX#9 subset shaped to Presentation Forms-B): correct joining and order with embedded Latin and numbers (UX-27 `[IMPLEMENTED]`). Extracted text holds 522 Presentation-Form-B characters and 0 base Arabic letters on page 1, so in-PDF search for `الصيانة` fails in viewers that do not normalise (UX-31 `[PARTIAL]`).
10. Header: the title is one 20 pt line, `slice(0,120)` (`:289`), drawn over the brand mark (`:280-289`); an 86-char title overflows the left page edge (UX-24 `[BROKEN]`). The timestamp is ISO UTC and there is no summary block (`:291-293`; UX-25 `[PARTIAL]`). Footer: disclaimer plus `i / n` page numbers (`:380-394`); limits 120k characters / 80 pages (`:35`) (UX-26, UX-34 `[IMPLEMENTED]`; UX harness, one warm run of 40 identical Q&A pairs: 80 messages → 22 pages, 174,936 B, 387 ms; the QA harness's n=12 warm runs on different content give p50 535.9 ms and 271,547 B, §6).

**DOWNLOAD**

11. Blob URL + anchor with `download`, revoked after 30 s (`chat-export.ts:73-84`); no `navigator.share` or new-tab fallback. `[UNVERIFIED]` iOS Safari tab and home-screen behaviour (UX-32).

**Break summary (2.3):** traceability (step 4), meaning (step 8); layout defect at step 10.

### 3.5 UI — Responsive, RTL, accessibility, visual system

Rows: 33 · BROKEN 6, IMPLEMENTED 12, MISSING 5, PARTIAL 8, UNVERIFIED 2 · severity P1 1, P2 13, P3 7, none 12

| ID | Requirement | Status | Evidence | Defect / Gap | Severity | Proposed Correction | Verification Test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UI-21 | Text and icon contrast in light theme (chat panel), per Articles 32-34: the second theme is a correctness surface | BROKEN | **Verified** (adversarial pass): Code confirmed: the header has a hard-coded dark gradient (SadeedCopilot.tsx:2354) and the backdrop is '#04070F' (373), while the title is text-foreground (2367) and the status text-muted-foreground. The CSS has no .light override for the header. Screenshot scratchpad/audit/UI/light-390-header-2x.png shows a near-invisible 'سَدِيد' and a white mute ring with no visible icon, which violates CLAUDE.md theme rule 6. I did not re-measure the pixel ratios. Pixel-sampled at 390 @2x in light mode: header title rgb(17,24,39) on rgb(5,20,38) gives 1.04:1, so 'سَدِيد' is effectively invisible. The status line (12px) is 3.89:1, below 4.5:1. Screenshot light-390-header-2x.png also shows the mute toggle (token --chip/--ink, SadeedMuteToggle.tsx:48) as a white ring with an invisible icon. The cause is that the panel surfaces are hard-coded dark while text follows theme tokens: SadeedPanelBackdrop style background '#04070F' (SadeedCopilot.tsx:373), header bg rgba(6,26,46,.76) (2354), composer bg rgba(3,15,29,…) (2653), scrim (2331). The assistant bubble stays readable (9.85:1) because it uses bg-background/75. | In light mode the chat header (product name, status, mute control) fails WCAG 1.4.3 and 1.4.11. | P1 | Move the header, composer and backdrop surfaces to tokens (--surface/--glass-*) with a .light variant, or scope dark tokens to .sadeed-window if the owner wants the panel always dark. Do not mix the two. | Playwright pixel-contrast test (probe2 method) on the header title, status and mute icon in light and dark: assert at least 4.5:1 for text and at least 3:1 for icons. |
| UI-02 | The composer stays reachable while the mobile virtual keyboard is open (visualViewport, dvh, interactive-widget) | UNVERIFIED | grep finds no visualViewport, interactive-widget or VirtualKeyboard anywhere in src. The viewport meta (__root.tsx:133) is only 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover'. The panel uses 86dvh inside a fixed inset-0 container. dvh does not shrink for the iOS keyboard, and Android Chrome defaults to resizes-visual. As a proxy I shrank the layout viewport to 390x480: the composer bottom sits at 431 < 480 (dark-390x480-panel.png). WebKit/iPhone Safari is not installed, so no real keyboard could be emulated. The composer is autofocused inside rAF on open (SadeedCopilot.tsx:579-582), which can pop the Android keyboard straight away. | Nothing handles the visual viewport, so on iOS the header and transcript probably pan off screen when the keyboard is open. Not measured on a device. | P2 | Size the dock from window.visualViewport (height plus offsetTop) using a CSS variable, or add interactive-widget=resizes-content to the viewport meta and test on iOS/Android. Consider skipping autofocus on coarse pointers. | Real-device check (iOS Safari plus Android Chrome): open the panel, tap the composer, and assert the composer and the close button are visible. Unit-level: a contract test that the dock height reads a visualViewport-driven variable. |
| UI-04 | Mixed Arabic/Latin (bidi) text in on-screen replies renders in the correct order | BROKEN | Paragraphs inside .prose compute to direction:rtl and unicode-bidi:isolate, with no dir attribute (measured via probe2 paraDir). Zoomed screenshot dark-390-bidi-bubble-2x.png: the English paragraph renders as '.(CLSD+DLFL)' with the period on the wrong side, and the date '2026-10-15.' splits as '2026-' / '.15-10'. The UAX#9 fix shipped today is in src/lib/reports/arabic-text.ts (PDF path only). ReactMarkdown output at SadeedCopilot.tsx:2142-2144 gets no dir=auto or plaintext handling. | On-screen replies do not get the bidi treatment the PDF export gets. Latin-led paragraphs and ISO dates or codes are misordered. | P2 | Add a [&_p,&_li,&_td]:[unicode-bidi:plaintext] rule (or dir="auto" via react-markdown components) to the message prose, and isolate code/date tokens. Reuse the arabic-text.ts logic for display where needed. | Playwright: render the mocked reply 'Work order WO-1 is pending (A+B).' and assert the computed unicode-bidi of the paragraph is plaintext, plus a visual snapshot. Unit: a contract test on the class string. |

### 3.6 SEC — Security, privacy, resilience

Rows: 26 · BROKEN 3, IMPLEMENTED 8, PARTIAL 13, UNVERIFIED 2 · severity P0 1, P1 1, P2 13, P3 5, none 6

| ID | Requirement | Status | Evidence | Defect / Gap | Severity | Proposed Correction | Verification Test |

| SEC-01 | Guest/anonymous readers must never receive personal identifiers (creator/technician names, employee numbers) — CLAUDE.md «الصيانة» §2 (masking via maint_orders_v) | BROKEN | **Verified** (adversarial pass): Measured in a scratch node run without printing values: maintenance-report.data.json has 249 rows, 249 with non-null created_by, 20 distinct values. Of those, 13 are numeric with 5–6 digits and 7 have the shape AA+10 digits. buildNotes puts 'أنشأه: <id>' in notes (maintenance-report.real.server.ts:107). formatWorkOrderRecord prints notes (recall.server.ts:215), notes are a match field (167), and the result reaches the guest chat (chat.ts:527-531). getWorkOrders (sec.functions.ts:532-551, optionalSupabaseAuth+resilient) returns the same rows to visitors. This conflicts with the owner decision of 2026-10-07 ② to hide employee numbers. Note: contract tests lock this behaviour (maintenance-report.contract.test.ts:72-73 expects all 249 notes to contain 'أنشأه:'; projects-maintenance-recall.test.ts:112), so a fix must update them. src/lib/exec/maintenance-report.real.server.ts:107 buildNotes pushes `أنشأه: ${r.created_by}` into `notes` (line 147) for REAL_SEC_WORK_ORDERS, sourced from git-tracked src/lib/exec/maintenance-report.data.json (measured: 249 rows, created_by non-null 249, 20 distinct values: 13 purely numeric of length 5–6, 7 shaped 2 letters + 10 digits). Guest chat branch src/routes/api/chat.ts:522-541 calls buildProductionDatasetsSnapshot(...,'anonymous') → src/lib/sadeed-context.server.ts:69 buildRecallBlock(question, REAL_SEC_PROJECTS, REAL_SEC_WORK_ORDERS, {provenance tags only}) → src/lib/sadeed/projects-maintenance-recall.server.ts:215 formatWorkOrderRecord prints `w.notes` verbatim, and notes are also a match field (line 167). src/lib/sec.functions.ts:530-548 (optionalSupabaseAuth + resilient) can return the same REAL_SEC_WORK_ORDERS (with notes) to guests on DB timeout/empty. Nothing in the path strips or redacts the identifiers. | This bundled export goes around the DB view masking. A visitor who asks about a specific work order gets the creator's employee or ID number in the model's context, and nothing tells the model to withhold it. The data file is also committed to git, against «البيانات الحقيقية لا تدخل git». | P0 | Remove created_by from the bundled notes (or drop the field from the JSON) whenever audience is anonymous or a non-member. Pass an audience flag into buildRecallBlock and formatWorkOrderRecord and strip notes for guests. Apply the same rule to the sec.functions fallback. RELEASE-BLOCKING. | Add a contract test: buildProductionDatasetsSnapshot(q containing a WO ref, ts, 'anonymous').contextBlock must contain no /أنشأه:/ and no /\b[A-Z]{2}\d{10}\b\|\b\d{5,6}\b/ creator values. As with INGEST-22, Host spoofing on the default Cloudflare target is platform-constrained and not measured, so P2. src/lib/sadeed/knowledge-upload.functions.ts:118-128 builds `hookUrl = new URL(req.url).origin + '/api/public/hooks/sadeed-docs-ingest'` (req = getRequest()) and sends header `x-cron-secret: secret` via fetch with no AbortSignal or timeout. | The destination host comes from the incoming request URL. TRUST_PROXY_HEADERS is not set anywhere in the repo (grep: only the two readers). When it is enabled, x-forwarded-for's first hop wins over cf-connecting-ip, and that hop is client-supplied. Public routes use cf-connecting-ip \|\| XFF \|\| 'unknown' (e.g. sadeed-ask.ts:118-124; rate-limit.server.ts:41-46 clientBucketKey). ## 4. Per-format ingestion support

Three entry points exist:

- **KU** — `/knowledge-upload`, the canonical path into the ingest hook. Text is extracted in the browser.
- **Chat** — composer attachments. A file is sent inline to Gemini for the current turn only. Text-like types (TXT, CSV, MD, JSON) are also deposited through the hook, after the reply.
- **Legacy** — `/sadeed-documents` → `ingestSadeedDocument` (defects in INGEST-30).

Rules that apply to every format:

- Extracted text is capped at 250,000 characters on KU, the hook and chat deposits; the legacy path allows 2,000,000 and silently truncates (INGEST-19, INGEST-30).
- No path checks magic bytes (INGEST-18).
- Knowledge-page (KU) and legacy uploads have no byte cap, and the storage buckets have no size or MIME limits (INGEST-19). Chat attachments are capped at 10 MiB per file / 20 MiB total on the server (8/16 MB on the client).
- No ingested chunk is retrievable in production (INGEST-34), and the KU page overstates readiness (INGEST-35).

| Format | What happens today | Status | IDs |
|---|---|---|---|
| PDF (text layer) | KU: pdf.js text, items joined by spaces, pages by blank lines, no page metadata; chunk citations carry no page. Chat: inline to Gemini for the current turn, never deposited. Legacy: a copy of the KU extractor. | PARTIAL | INGEST-01, INGEST-27, INGEST-36 |
| PDF (scanned / image-only) | KU throws "No extractable text" (`knowledge-upload.tsx:151`); no OCR dependency. Chat: Gemini sees the pages for that turn only. | MISSING | INGEST-02 |
| DOCX | KU: mammoth raw text; tables, headings and lists flattened, images dropped. Chat: rejected (absent from both client and server allowlists). | PARTIAL | INGEST-03 |
| DOC (legacy Word) | KU and legacy reject it with an English-first "Unsupported file type"; chat rejects it. No conversion, no Arabic guidance. | MISSING | INGEST-04 |
| TXT | KU and chat read with `File.text()` (UTF-8 only); Windows-1256 Arabic becomes U+FFFD with no detection. Chat sends inline and deposits. | PARTIAL | INGEST-05 |
| RTF | No branch. If the browser reports `text/rtf`, raw control words may be ingested (browser MIME not measured). Chat rejects. | MISSING | INGEST-06 |
| Markdown | Raw text on KU; chunking ignores headings. Chat sends inline as `text/markdown` and deposits; Gemini acceptance not measured. | PARTIAL | INGEST-07 |
| HTML | KU accepts it by drag-and-drop only and ingests raw markup, `<script>` included. Chat rejects. | PARTIAL | INGEST-08 |
| CSV | KU by drop only, raw text; legacy picker too. Chat sends inline and deposits. Rows split with no header repeat; cp1256 Excel CSVs garbled. | PARTIAL | INGEST-09 |
| XLSX / XLS | Legacy page only (SheetJS `sheet_to_csv` per sheet). KU and chat reject it. | PARTIAL | INGEST-10 |
| JSON | Raw text on KU (drop only) and legacy, no key-path flattening. Chat sends inline as `application/json` and deposits; Gemini acceptance not measured. | PARTIAL | INGEST-11 |
| XML | KU ingests raw markup on drop. Chat rejects. | PARTIAL | INGEST-12 |
| PPTX | No branch anywhere; rejected. | MISSING | INGEST-13 |
| PNG / JPEG / WEBP | Chat: inline to Gemini for the current turn only, never deposited; later turns see metadata only. KU rejects. | PARTIAL | INGEST-14, UX-18 |
| GIF / HEIC | Rejected in chat and on KU. | MISSING (rejected on every path; INGEST-14's PARTIAL covers PNG/JPEG/WEBP only) | INGEST-14 |
| Audio (recorded briefings) | No attachment type. Microphone transcription exists (`/api/sadeed/transcribe`), but transcripts are not ingested. | MISSING | INGEST-15 |
| Source code (`.js`, `.py`, …) | KU ingests any `text/*` file raw when dropped; chat rejects. No stated policy. | PARTIAL | INGEST-16 |

## 5. Architecture findings

Cross-cutting themes (detail by area follows the placeholder):

1. **No single retrieval contract.** Three live retrieval stacks do not share a contract: 768-d `aria_match_*` over the renamed `finn_*` tables (broken in production), the governed 1536-d `retrieveWithEvidenceGate` → `sec_kb_hybrid_search` (reached only from tools), and legacy 768-d `sec_kb_search`. A fourth, unapplied `20260728*` stack sits behind a flag. Ingestion writes into the first, which nothing can read (RAG-01, CHAT-14, RAG-07, SEC-08, INGEST-34).
2. **A buffered answer path behind a regex gateway.** The safe-output gateway (`finalize.ts`) works on the whole text, which is why streaming was removed. Its over-broad `RE_PHONE` rule (`finalize.ts:174`) rewrites ISO dates, decimal series and long reference numbers in any answer (INGEST-37). Separately, it is not connected to tool execution or to attachments, so it relabels executed actions and judges citations against only part of the evidence (CHAT-02, CHAT-17, RAG-11).
3. **Failure is silent and telemetry undercounts.** RPC errors become `console.warn` plus empty context; `rag_query_log` is never written on the main path; `first_token_ms` is a constant null; `latency_ms` starts after the pre-model stages; `/api/chat/health` reflects environment variables only; the CI RAG preflight validates the unapplied schema and exits 0 without Postgres (RAG-21, QA-14, QA-15, QA-16, QA-18, QA-02).
4. **Data paths that bypass the database's guarantees.** Guest chat context is built from git-bundled exports, outside `maint_orders_v` masking (SEC-01). Storage defence is policy-only (no bucket limits). Security helpers exist but are unwired: `file-inspection.server.ts`, `rateLimitRag` (SEC-13, QA-07, SEC-08).
5. **Claims ahead of code.** UI copy, code comments and governing docs describe behaviour that does not exist: "can now cite", deposit of every file, the layer-2 provider cooldown, a quarantine flow, and a 21-item RAG anchor (the code holds 41) (INGEST-35, UX-17, CHAT-08, INGEST-39, RAG-25).
6. **Token and motion discipline broken on the chat surface.** `SadeedCopilot.tsx` mixes hard-coded dark surfaces with theme-token text, and later `!important` decorative rules in global CSS (`styles.css:4650-4671`, e.g. `button > svg { hx-halo-breathe infinite !important }`) override the reduced-motion floor. The accessibility floors work as defaults, not guarantees (UI-21, UI-22, UI-19).

### CHAT — Chat critical path, streaming, routing, context

- Critical path (code trace, HEAD ea9a947): SadeedCopilot.submit (SadeedCopilot.tsx:1288-1328) calls chat.sendMessage directly (bypassing sadeedBrain), then useChat singleton (sadeed-provider.tsx:51), then DefaultChatTransport with windowChatMessages(20) (sadeed-service.ts:99-128), then sadeedAuthedFetch (Bearer, falling back to anonymous on 401/403), then POST /api/chat (chat.ts:252). There: kill switch → validateChatRequest (body) → [auth: getUser → org_members] → check_rate_limit → quota global → quota identity → scope guard / fast recall / instant data (fixed stream) → [auth: snapshot(15 queries, 2.5s race) ‖ RAG(embed→aria_match_*×3→finn_memory update)] or [guest: production datasets] → [HCIS finn_knowledge read] → [deep: council 50s] → tools(31) → adaptive prefs → runtime clock prepended → generateText(Gemini 3.6 flash, ≤12 steps, maxRetries 2) → finalizeSadeedOutput → sealEnvelope → single text-delta plus metadata → fire-and-forget persistence.
- Latency MODEL (from code, not measured). Guest model turn: T_first_byte ≈ upload + 3×RTT_db (rate, quota-global, quota-id) + [cold isolate: dataset module load/parse] + T_gemini(full buffered generation: prefill of a system prompt of about 51-61k chars, then all output tokens; 700-token cap when replyMode=voice, the default) + finalize (sync, CPU). Authenticated turn: upload + RTT_auth(getUser) + RTT_db(org_members) + 3×RTT_db + max(snapshot ≤2.5s, RAG[flag on]: RTT_gemini_embed (≤3×15s) + RTT_rpc + RTT_db_update) + [HCIS: RTT_db] + RTT_db(prefs) + Σ_steps(T_gemini_step + tool DB RTTs) (≤12 steps). Fast path: guest 3 RTTs, authenticated 5 RTTs, plus a match under 1ms. Voice time to first audio = T_first_byte + first-sentence TTS. Because the response is buffered, time to first byte equals total turn time on every model path.
- Network vs local stages: network = Supabase Auth getUser; PostgREST org_members; 3 RPCs (check_rate_limit); 15 snapshot queries (parallel); Gemini embed; 3 aria_match RPCs; finn_memory update; finn_knowledge HCIS read; finn_adaptive_preferences read; Gemini generate (×steps); tool DB calls. Local = JSON validation, sanitizeHistory, scope/fast-recall/instant matchers, production datasets block, convertToModelMessages, finalize/seal.
- The 'safe output gateway' (finalize.ts) is regex-based over the full model text (exec-claim, secret leak, exfiltration, KPI/time/evidence rules). This is the root reason streaming was removed. Per-token streaming is incompatible with it as written; per-sentence streaming would need a retract/replace path for whole-text rules.
- The output gateway is not connected to tool execution: chat.ts never reads gen.steps/toolResults and passes no receipts. Executed writes can therefore be relabelled 'requires approval' (measured on 2 of 3 phrasings), and write tools run without any confirmation gate inside the turn.
- The chat path holds two divergent retrieval systems: pre-model vector RAG (768-d, aria_match_*, user-only filter, broken in production with 42P01 per lead, swallowed errors, no rag_query_log) and the search_knowledge tool via retrieveWithEvidenceGate (hybrid sec_kb_hybrid_search, evidence gate, rag_query_log). The code constitution Art 31 mandates only the latter.
- Persistence designed for continuity is disconnected: the singleton chat id 'sadeed-singleton' fails the UUID check, so threads/messages are never written; /assistant/$threadId ignores its param; no client storage. Memory (finn_memory) depends on SADEED_GEMINI_RAG_READY plus a likely-broken RPC. aria_conversations/aria_learning_log have no writer.
- Resilience layers vs code: layer 1 (no-key local reply) exists; layer 2 client retry exists but is misclassified, and the server cooldown/alternate provider does not exist (Gemini-only policy); layer 3 voice continuity exists. A DB/RPC outage still yields a 503 through fail-closed rate limit/quota, even for no-model answers.
- Client 30s timeout is ineffective for chat because AI SDK v6 always supplies init.signal (index.mjs:14208/14619) and sadeedAuthedFetch prefers it (sadeed-service.ts:46,59). Combined with the unbounded generateText and DB calls, a hang has no upper bound.
- Prompt economics (measured locally, chars not tokens): base SYSTEM_PROMPT 42,600 chars + runtime block 2,485 + guest datasets 5,912-16,191, plus 31 tools (14,652 chars of description and schema) for authenticated turns, plus live data/RAG up to 48k each. A volatile clock block at offset 0 prevents prefix reuse.
- Minor correctness: runtime block labels every authenticated user user_role 'manager' (chat.ts:758) regardless of orgRole. The instant-data module header still says guests are excluded (instant-data-answers.server.ts:27-31), contrary to the code and the 2026-09-03 order. Citations/coverage metadata are sent but not rendered by the copilot (no 'citations' use in SadeedCopilot.tsx). grounded=false (client-controllable) re-enables the simulation line for KPI questions via finalize (finalize.ts RE_KPI branch), despite simulation being removed from chat.

### INGEST — Knowledge ingestion pipeline & per-format support

- Trace (canonical): /knowledge-upload extracts text in the browser (pdf.js, mammoth, File.text), uploads the original straight to the private aria-documents bucket (best effort, errors ignored), then calls enqueueKnowledgeIngest (requireSupabaseAuth). That inserts a finn_documents row (status 'pending'), runs a non-atomic dedupe, and makes a synchronous HTTP self-call with x-cron-secret to /api/public/hooks/sadeed-docs-ingest. The hook (service role) runs chunkText (1600/200), then Gemini embedContent per chunk (768 dims, concurrency 4), upserts finn_document_chunks, and marks the document 'ready'. Only extracted text crosses to the server; the server never reads the stored bytes.
- Search is the dead end: the only chunk-level consumer is aria_match_documents. It is reached by chat (gatherRagContext, gated by SADEED_GEMINI_RAG_READY) and by /sadeed-documents search, and it fails in prod with 42P01. So all ingestion currently produces zero retrievable knowledge. The working hybrid search (sec_kb_hybrid_search) reads sec_knowledge_articles, which has no file-upload path; it is fed only by seeding plus kbEmbedMissing.
- Three live writers plus one dead one write divergent corpora. (1) The hook path: 250k cap, no-loss chunker, dedupe. (2) The legacy ingestSadeedDocument behind /sadeed-documents (still linked from marketplace and enterprise search): 2M cap, 900/120 chunker that silently truncates past 400 chunks, null embeddings allowed with status 'ready', no revision_hash, chunk org_id left to the default org. (3) Chat depositTextFiles, which reuses (1) for text types only. (4) Dead: supabase/functions/rag-ingest targets rag_documents/rag_chunks at 1536 dims, with no caller and tables absent in prod.
- Chat attachments are model-only and ephemeral. Allowlisted PDF, image, text, CSV, MD and JSON files go as inline base64 data URLs to Gemini for the current turn. Later turns see only '[Attachment metadata]'. Only text types are deposited, and only after the reply completes. No signature validation: the existing src/lib/security/file-inspection.server.ts is unused, and the aria-attachments-quarantine bucket and aria_attachment_scans table have no code references.
- The answer-path gateway collides with attachments (measured). finalizeSadeedOutput's regulatory gate never counts attachments as evidence, so summaries of attached SEC documents are refused. Its RE_PHONE PII redaction rewrites ISO dates, decimal series and reference numbers to '[هاتف محجوب]'. The pre-model instant-data short-circuit ignores file parts.
- Status semantics overclaim. 'Citation Ready' is defined as 'all chunks embedded' (getKnowledgeChunkStats) and the page prints 'SADEED can now cite this document', yet no retrieval check exists. 'Async/enqueue' is a synchronous request chain: for a 250k-char document that is 179–249 sequential-at-width-4 embedding calls, with no job record, sweeper or document-level retry.
- Limits are unified only for extracted text (250,000 chars, enforced at four layers). Byte limits are absent on the knowledge page, the advertised 50MB is unenforced, and prod buckets have file_size_limit and allowed_mime_types NULL. Chat client and server caps differ (8/16MB versus 10/20MiB), and the 24MB body cap relies on the Content-Length header.
- The hook invocation sends CRON_HOOK_SECRET to an origin derived from the incoming request URL instead of a pinned base URL or an in-process call. Exploitability depends on the platform's Host handling, which was not measured.
- Production schema for the chunk upsert target is unknown. The hook's ON CONFLICT (document_id, chunk_index) needs a unique index that no migration creates. If prod's index is non-unique, every hook ingest fails with 42P10. The 8 'ready' prod documents could have come from the legacy path; model_version distribution would show which.

### RAG — Retrieval, grounding, citations, prompt injection

- Three retrieval stacks coexist and do not share a contract. (1) Automatic chat RAG: gatherRagContext uses 768-dim legacy embeddings and the aria_match_* RPCs over the finn_* tables. It is vector-only, fails closed on flag/org, and RPC errors only produce console.warn (src/lib/sadeed/rag.server.ts:82-213). (2) Governed path: retrieveWithEvidenceGate embeds at 1536 dims and calls sec_kb_hybrid_search (RRF over dense, FTS and trigram) over sec_knowledge_articles, with an evidence gate. It is reached only from the search_knowledge chat tool and the rag.search_verified agent tool (src/lib/sadeed/rag/retrieve.server.ts). (3) Legacy sec_kb_search uses 768-dim kb-embed (kbSemanticSearch, agent kb.search). A fourth, unapplied stack (rag-retrieve edge function → rag_hybrid_search, 20260728*) sits behind a VITE flag. The runtime constitution (src/lib/sadeed/constitution.ts item 31) says ALL retrieval must go through retrieveWithEvidenceGate at 1536 dims. Stacks 1 and 3 violate that.
- The ingestion pipeline shipped today (knowledge-ingest-core, sadeed-docs-ingest hook, knowledge-upload page) writes finn_documents/finn_document_chunks at vector(768). The only retrieval reader of those chunks is aria_match_documents, which fails in prod with 42P01. Ingestion therefore feeds a dead end: 8 documents and 18 chunks that no answer can ever use.
- chat.ts has a control-flow bug: the guest/no-live-data branch sets liveDataAvailable=true (chat.ts:532), which makes the 'UNTRUSTED TENANT KNOWLEDGE' injection (chat.ts:544-552) unreachable. RAG text reaches the model only when the authenticated caller also has operational data. Its citations are still handed to the output gateway as available evidence (chat.ts:843).
- Citation enforcement lives in the output gateway (finalize.ts:440-460). It is regex-triggered (regulatory claims only), it lets invented tags through whenever one real tag is present (measured), it refuses correct bare 'SEC-xx' citations (measured), and it ignores tool-sourced citations because evidence.available = ragCitations only. No UI renders citations (SadeedCopilot.tsx reads only metadata.envelope, for speech).
- Injection defense is layered but uneven. Untrusted blocks are delimited, <> is escaped and an instruction hierarchy is stated (chat.ts:109-113, 516-551). sanitizeRetrievedContent and safeExcerpt are applied only to tool outputs. The auto-RAG contextBlock and uploaded content are not sanitized, and finalize's retrievedDocs injection check is never fed by chat.ts.
- The gap-capture path is both broken and non-constitutional. search_knowledge calls rag_record_gap_for_org with `.rpc(...).catch(...)`. supabase-js builders have no .catch (measured: TypeError), so every non-passed gate outcome turns into a tool exception. The target table rag_gaps is absent in prod. The call sits inside a user request path, which Article 26 forbids.
- Isolation in retrieval relies on RLS rather than explicit predicates. gatherRagContext gates on orgId but never passes it. aria_match_documents filters by user only (repo body). aria_match_knowledge is unfiltered. sec_kb_search has no org parameter. sec_kb_hybrid_search treats a NULL org as 'all orgs', and in prod anon has EXECUTE on it.
- Observability is absent: rag_query_log has 0 rows in prod. The auto-RAG path never logs, early returns skip logging, and insert {error} results are ignored. firstTokenAt is a const null (chat.ts:718), so no TTFT is recorded either.
- The measurement and gate machinery (src/lib/rag/evaluation.ts, tuning.ts, supabase/preflight/rag_measure.sql) is sound on Article 27 semantics (null rather than 0) but measures an unapplied schema (rag_hybrid_search). No runner targets the production RPCs, so recall and citation precision for production are 'not measured'.

### UX — Composer (mic, upload, PDF export), PDF quality, knowledge library

- One composer: /sadeed, /assistant and /assistant/$threadId (src/routes/_authenticated/*) all render CanonicalSadeedRoute, which portals the single SadeedCopilot (SadeedCopilot.tsx:2815-2820). Its mic button only toggles voice conversation mode (2716-2742); there is no dictate-to-draft mode.
- Voice capture order: MediaRecorder with local VAD, an RMS analyser and a 30 s cap (1576-1727), then transcribeVoiceBlob calls /api/sadeed/transcribe and falls back to /api/public/sadeed-transcribe on 401/403/429/5xx (1525-1541). The browser SpeechRecognition path (ar-SA) is used only without MediaRecorder or outside conversation mode. transcribeVoiceBlob returns early unless convModeRef is true (1512).
- Chat attachments are never stored. They travel as inline base64 data-URL parts in the /api/chat POST (fileToPart, sadeed-attachments.ts:81-99). Both client (chat-window.ts) and server (-chat-guard.ts:123-128) reduce older turns to metadata text. Text-like files are additionally deposited into finn_documents via enqueueKnowledgeIngest, and only after chat.sendMessage resolves (SadeedCopilot.tsx:1315-1316).
- The content inspector inspectSadeedAttachment (src/lib/security/file-inspection.server.ts) is used only by its own test. The chat guard trusts the declared mediaType and the data-URL prefix.
- Export pipeline: UIMessage → displayTextOf/exportableOf (SadeedCopilot.tsx:344-359) → toPdfMessages (chat-export.ts:19) → buildChatPdf (pdf-lib + fontkit, embedded IBM Plex Sans Arabic subset, non-embedded standard Helvetica for Latin; pdffonts emb=no) → blob and anchor download. There is no sources stage. The server's metadata.citations (chat.ts:972-976) is dropped by the client, which reads only metadata.envelope (SadeedCopilot.tsx:1089).
- sanitizePdfText is an allow-list (chat-pdf.ts:63-78) that deletes any character outside the Arabic and WinAnsi ranges. Deleting rather than substituting is the root cause of the sign and comparison loss in UX-22.
- The chat server's safe-output path emits the whole reply as a single text-delta (chat.ts:965-970), so the composer's 'streaming' is one chunk on that path.
- Two document UIs share the same server functions: /knowledge-upload (upload, status, delete) and /sadeed-documents (list, delete, semantic search). Both list through RLS only (documents.functions.ts:54-65). Search and chat RAG both depend on aria_match_documents (documents.functions.ts:202, rag.server.ts:105), which is broken in production.
- Bundle constraint: total JS is 9.87/10.00 MiB (about 133 KB headroom). The PDF engine is already lazy (chat-export dynamic import, chat-pdf imports pdf-lib and fontkit dynamically). Any table or PDF improvements must stay in lazy chunks.

### UI — Responsive, RTL, accessibility, visual system

- The chat panel (SadeedCopilot.tsx:2336-2820) is portalled to <body> as a centered dock (z-110) over a blurred scrim (z-105) on every breakpoint. It is declared role=complementary, not as a dialog, so it looks modal but is not modal semantically. That accounts for UI-12, UI-13 and UI-14.
- /sadeed and /assistant open the same SadeedCopilot panel (measured asideOpen=true), so the chat surface is a single component. Fixes in SadeedCopilot.tsx apply to all three entry points.
- The panel mixes two color systems: hard-coded dark surfaces (backdrop #04070F at :373, header/composer rgba gradients at :2354/:2653) and theme-token text (text-foreground, --ink/--chip in SadeedMuteToggle). Dark mode is coherent; light mode flips the text to dark ink on the still-dark surfaces, giving a 1.04:1 title. This is a whole class of bug, not a single defect.
- Accessibility floors are set once at the token layer (styles.css:2521-2590): Arabic line-height, a 44px coarse / 40px fine touch floor via :where() with zero specificity, a focus-visible ring, and a global reduced-motion kill. Later decorative rules with !important and higher specificity (styles.css:4650-4658, button > svg { hx-halo-breathe infinite !important }) override the reduced-motion floor, so the floors work as defaults, not guarantees.
- Transcript rendering is memoised react-markdown with remark-gfm (no MessageResponse), as the constitution requires. Bidi is left to the inherited dir=rtl, with no per-paragraph plaintext isolation. The UAX#9 work shipped today covers only the PDF path (src/lib/reports/arabic-text.ts).
- /knowledge-upload is a standalone, English-led admin page outside the nexios-ui-patterns state conventions: no error branch, no deadline, no live region, and a destructive action without confirmation.
- Local preview method (reproducible, read-only): copy .output to the scratchpad, patch only the scratch copy's compatibility_date to 2026-08-06 (the local workerd supports up to 2026-08-06), run wrangler dev on 127.0.0.1:4319 under a restart supervisor (wrangler crashed once mid-run), and in Playwright abort every non-127.0.0.1 request and mock /api/chat with an AI SDK v6 UI-message SSE stream. No production calls were made from the browser. The repo was left unmodified (git status clean).

### SEC — Security, privacy, resilience

- Two parallel retrieval stacks. /api/chat uses gatherRagContext (rag.server.ts: 768-dim, aria_match_* RPCs, vector-only, no evidence gate, no rag_query_log, no PII sanitize). Tools and the orchestrator use retrieveWithEvidenceGate (rag/retrieve.server.ts: 1536-dim, sec_kb_hybrid_search RRF, injection screen, logging). The constitution (Art. 31) names only the second. In prod both are inert: the first because the aria_match_* bodies point at renamed tables (42P01), the second because SADEED_GEMINI_RAG_READY gates it and rag_query_log has 0 rows.
- RAG failure is silent by design. gatherRagContext turns RPC errors into console.warn plus empty context (rag.server.ts:108-110), so the prod 42P01 never reaches the user, telemetry or rag_query_log. Breakage is undetectable without log scraping.
- Guest chat context is built from git-bundled 'real' export JSON (maintenance-report.data.json, projects, event report) instead of the masked DB view. Whatever privacy masking the DB applies (maint_orders_v) does not cover this path, and that is the root cause of SEC-01.
- Tenant scoping in /api/chat is derived server-side: org comes from org_members matched with app_metadata.current_org, and RAG fails closed on ambiguity. The service-role orchestrator path (agents.ts:418) shifts isolation from RLS to the SQL body's _org_id predicate.
- Privileged ingest runs through an HTTP self-call carrying CRON_HOOK_SECRET to an origin derived from the request (knowledge-upload.functions.ts:118). The ingest core is already pure (knowledge-ingest-core.ts), so an in-process call would remove both the secret hop and the missing-timeout risk.
- Rate limiting is DB-backed (check_rate_limit through supabaseAdmin) and fails closed (503). The chat guest identity collapses to one global 'shared' key unless TRUST_PROXY_HEADERS=true, and when enabled it prefers spoofable XFF over cf-connecting-ip, the reverse of the order used by sadeed-incident.ts and the public routes.
- Security helpers exist but are unwired: src/lib/security/file-inspection.server.ts (zip-ratio/Office structure inspection) is imported only by its test, and rateLimitRag (rag/sanitize.server.ts:70) has no callers.
- Storage defense is entirely policy-based. Prod buckets have no size or MIME limits, and the knowledge-upload client uploads the raw file with no size cap before server validation of the extracted text.

### QA — Tests, observability, baselines

- The /api/chat model path is fully buffered. generateText (chat.ts:795) runs to completion with stopWhen stepCountIs(12) and maxRetries 2. The output then passes the finalizeSadeedOutput gateway and goes out as one text-delta (chat.ts:965-970). All fixed paths (local guaranteed reply chat.ts:285, scope-guard, fast-recall and instant-data chat.ts:422) are also single deltas. So the user's TTFT equals total server time; this is a deliberate 'Option A' (chat.ts:765-768).
- The pre-model chain for an authenticated, grounded, text-mode turn has 11 awaits on every turn, plus 2 conditional ones: validateChatRequest/request.json (295), auth.getUser (331), org_members (345), enforceRateLimit RPC (380), enforceAiQuota with 2 sequential check_rate_limit RPCs (390; ai-quota.server.ts:182,204), instant-data import (462), module imports (489), Promise.all[snapshot raced vs deadline, rag: embed then 3 aria_match_* RPCs] (493), conditional HCIS finn_knowledge read (574), conditional deep council runAgentMission with a 50 s timeout (650), tools import (695), loadAdaptivePreferenceBlock (725), convertToModelMessages (798). That is 7 strictly sequential network round-trips (8 with RAG on, 9 with HCIS). A guest turn has 3 (rate plus 2 quota) before the production snapshot.
- Telemetry undercounts. startAt is set at chat.ts:717, after every pre-model stage, so finn_interactions.latency_ms (chat.ts:889) and the 'buffered finish' log omit auth, rate, quota, snapshot and RAG time. first_token_ms is always null (const at :718). finn_interactions has no request_id. Guest turns write no interaction or incident rows (the `if (userId)` gate at :821 and :861).
- Retrieval observability is disconnected. rag_query_log is written only by retrieveWithEvidenceGate.finish (retrieve.server.ts:136-157), on the search_knowledge tool and agent paths. The default chat retrieval, gatherRagContext (rag.server.ts:82-110), uses a different stack (768-dim embeddings.server.ts:9, aria_match_* RPCs, no injection screen, no sanitizeRetrievedContent, no rateLimitRag, no log). That contradicts the constitution's Article 31 as stated in src/lib/sadeed/constitution.ts:46 (1536 dims, every retrieval through retrieveWithEvidenceGate, every attempt logged).
- The RAG embedding call does have a deadline: httpJson with timeoutMs 15_000 and retries 2 (gemini-embeddings.server.ts:98-108). It is not bound to request.signal, and the total worst case including retries was not measured.
- /api/chat/health (src/routes/api/chat.health.ts) only reflects environment variables (ok: true hard-coded, rag_ready = env flag). It cannot detect the production 42P01 on aria_match_documents. Production-check does not call it: ROUTES defaults to '/,/sec,/sec/governance' (check-production.mjs:25).
- CI coverage: ci.yml runs static checks, vitest, npm audit, build plus the budget, contrast, and rag-preflight. The rag-preflight targets the unapplied 20260728 migrations and exits 0 with no Postgres (rag-preflight.sh:21-23). The security-gate runs only 2 of 14 Playwright specs, gated by secrets. production-check.yml covers HTML and UI floors only. No workflow benchmarks latency, TTFT, retrieval or error rates. Several 'contract' tests (chat-latency.contract.test.ts, rag-tool-dependency.contract.test.ts, sadeed-attachments.test.ts) use readFileSync to read source text: they guard structure, not runtime behaviour.
- The test inventory, by journey. Knowledge upload→ask: ingest pure functions only (knowledge-ingest-core.test.ts), no retrieval or answer test. Arabic/English: deterministic layers only. Streaming: structural contract only. Mic/STT: none for capture or transcribe fallback; TTS and voice config are covered. Upload: client/server mirror covered (sadeed-attachments, -chat-guard, chat-window.e2e), storage limits not. PDF: covered (chat-pdf, chat-export ×2, arabic-text, pdf-rich). Isolation: live test skipped (rls-isolation skipIf !LIVE), static coverage plus the rag-edge-core negative test. Injection: none. Citations: rank-metric fixtures only; the live evaluation is skipped.
- src/lib/security/file-inspection.server.ts (the hypothesis gave the wrong path, src/lib/sadeed/...) is imported only by its own test, so it is dead in production.
- Measured local cost notes. chunkText is negligible (≤3.9 ms max even at 250k characters), but the 200-character overlap inflates embedding input by about 1.18–1.21× (sumChunkChars/chars). A 250k-character document yields 226–241 chunks, below MAX_CHUNKS_PER_DOCUMENT=400, so 250k is not cap-bound. buildChatPdf scales about linearly: about 31 ms for 2 messages, 139 ms for 20, 536 ms for 80 (p50, Node on 4 vCPU). It runs client-side via downloadChatPdf, so on mobile main threads it is probably slower, but that was not measured.
- Not measurable from this session, per Article 27: production TTFT, end-to-end chat latency, retrieval latency, indexing (embedding) time, throughput, and production error or timeout rates. The reasons: there is no production network (sandbox policy), no provider keys, rag_query_log has 0 rows, first_token_ms is always null, and latency_ms excludes the pre-model stages.
- Proposed measurement, not implemented. (a) Server instrumentation in /api/chat: t0 at handler entry; a mark() around validate, getUser, org_members, rate, quota (both RPCs), snapshot, rag.embed, rag.rpc, hcis, prefs, model, finalize. Emit a Server-Timing header and one structured JSON log per request with requestId. Persist total_ms and pre_model_ms plus request_id (owner-gated columns). (b) Make gatherRagContext write rag_query_log (route 'chat-auto', correlation_id=requestId, latency, hits, max_similarity) and log the flag-off and no-org rejections. (c) TTFT: in the client transport, record performance.now() at fetch start and at the first SSE chunk, and post it with requestId; if a sentence-streaming flag is added, set the server firstTokenAt in streamText onChunk. (d) Ingest hook: log extract, chunk, embed and insert durations, chunk count and embed failures per document. (e) A GitHub Actions workflow 'latency-probe.yml' (workflow_dispatch plus a nightly cron, run after Publish): sign in a synthetic user with the existing E2E_TEST_EMAIL/PASSWORD secrets; POST N≥30 fixed Arabic and N≥30 fixed English questions to /api/chat with x-aria-request-id; record TTFB, total and X-SADEED-Reply-Source; call /api/chat/health; write p50/p95 and error/timeout counts to the job summary and an artifact; enforce thresholds only after a baseline exists. (f) A server-side alternative: a pg_cron or SQL view computing daily p50/p95 from finn_interactions.latency_ms and rag_query_log.latency_ms, and error rate as finn_incidents(model_error)/finn_interactions.


---

## 6. Baselines

Every value in the baseline table was measured locally or derived from code reading on HEAD `ea9a947` (the rows say which); none was measured in production, and rows whose value is "not measured" say why. It covers:

- **Prompt economics:** static `SYSTEM_PROMPT` 42,600 chars; runtime block 2,485; guest datasets block 5,912–16,191; 31 tools carrying 14,652 chars of description and schema.
- **Pre-model chain (static, from code reading):** 11 unconditional awaits and 7 strictly sequential network round trips for an authenticated turn.
- **Gateway probes:** executed-action relabelling, attachment-answer corruption, citation validation.
- **Ingestion:** chunker output and timing (p95 ≤ 3.183 ms over n=25 runs (QA); single runs 1.8–3.4 ms (INGEST)), overlap inflation, legacy truncation, dedupe-hash sensitivity, hash timing; embedding calls per max-size document are derived from chunk counts, not measured.
- **PDF:** build time (QA harness, n=12 warm: p50 31 / 139 / 536 ms for 2 / 20 / 80 messages; the 80-message PDF is 271,547 B), glyph coverage, sanitizer fidelity, text extractability. The UX harness recorded one warm run of 80 messages (40 identical Q&A pairs) at 387 ms, 174,936 B and 22 pages; the two harnesses use different content.
- **UI geometry and accessibility:** contrast in both themes, focus and Escape behaviour, reduced motion, from Playwright on a local preview with `/api/chat` mocked.
- **Data and gates:** the shape-only scan of the maintenance export; targeted test runs.

None of these numbers is a production latency.

### Not measured in production

| Metric | Why it is not measured | Instrumentation to add | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| TTFT (time to first SSE chunk and to first visible text), guest and authenticated | Production domains blocked from the sandbox; no provider keys; the response is buffered, so first byte = whole turn; `first_token_ms` is a constant null (`chat.ts:718, 890`); the client's `firstTokenAt` is the arrival of the single delta (`SadeedCopilot.tsx:1118`) (QA-15, CHAT-18) | Client transport records `performance.now()` at fetch start and at the first SSE chunk and posts it with `requestId`; once streaming exists, the server sets `firstTokenAt` on the first delta (`streamText` `onChunk`); until then store null with an explicit reason | `src/lib/sadeed-core/services/sadeed-service.ts`; `src/routes/api/chat.ts`; `finn_interactions.first_token_ms` |
| Per-stage server latency (validate, getUser, org_members, rate, quota ×2, snapshot, rag.embed, rag.rpc, hcis, prefs, model, finalize) and end-to-end turn time | `startAt` is set at `chat.ts:717`, after all pre-model stages, so `latency_ms` under-reports; no `Server-Timing`; `finn_interactions` has no `request_id` (QA-14, QA-13, QA-21) | `t0` at handler entry; a `mark()` per stage; `Server-Timing` header; one structured JSON log line per request with `requestId`; persist `total_ms`, `pre_model_ms`, `request_id` (owner-gated columns, §9.3) | `src/routes/api/chat.ts`; `finn_interactions`; log drain |
| Retrieval latency | Chat retrieval fails with 42P01 and the error is swallowed; `gatherRagContext` never logs; `retrieveWithEvidenceGate` returns before `finish()` for missing org and flag-off; insert `{error}` is ignored; `rag_query_log` has 0 rows (RAG-21, QA-16, RAG-23) | Log every attempt including early returns (`route 'chat-auto'`, `correlation_id = requestId`, latency, hits, `max_similarity`); check the insert's `{error}`; race retrieval against a fixed stage budget | `src/lib/sadeed/rag.server.ts`; `src/lib/sadeed/rag/retrieve.server.ts:113-157`; `rag_query_log` |
| Retrieval quality (recall@5, citation precision) | The harness `rag_measure.sql` targets the unapplied `rag_hybrid_search`; probes are verbatim seed titles (self-retrieval); `citationAccuracy` is hit@1 rank, not answer-citation precision; `evaluateRetrieval` has no non-test caller (RAG-25, QA-11) | Port the harness to the retrieval RPC chosen under §9.4; add paraphrased probes derived from seed content; measure answer-citation precision separately; record results in an eval ledger | `src/lib/rag/evaluation.ts`, `tuning.ts`; `supabase/preflight/rag_measure.sql` |
| Indexing time per document and embedding failure rate | No per-document timings; whether the hook logs them was not verified; ingestion runs inside one synchronous request (179–249 embedding calls for a max-size document) (QA-24, INGEST-21) | Hook logs extract, chunk, embed and insert durations, chunk count and embed failures with `document_id` and `requestId`; optional `processing_ms` column (owner-gated) | `sadeed-docs-ingest.ts` hook; `finn_documents` |
| Throughput under concurrency | No load or probe workflow exists; all guests share one bucket (12 per 300 s) unless `TRUST_PROXY_HEADERS=true`, which would cap any guest test; probes consume provider quota and write rows (QA-20, CHAT-22) | `latency-probe.yml` (workflow_dispatch, later nightly after Publish): a synthetic authenticated user (existing `E2E_TEST_*` secrets) posts N ≥ 30 Arabic and N ≥ 30 English fixed questions with `x-aria-request-id`, at stated concurrency levels; record p50/p95 TTFB and total; thresholds only after a baseline exists | `.github/workflows/latency-probe.yml` (proposed); preview first, production after approval (§9.15) |
| Error and timeout rates (model_error, 429/503, client-side timeouts) | No aggregation or alerting; guest turns write no interaction or incident rows (`chat.ts:821, 861`); a hung turn raises no error at all (QA-17, CHAT-10) | Structured logs to a log drain with alerts on `model_error` and 429/503 counts; a hashed anonymous identity; a daily SQL view `finn_incidents(model_error) / finn_interactions` | `src/routes/api/chat.ts`; Cloudflare Logpush or similar (owner choice); SQL view |
| Post-response write loss on Workers | Unawaited persistence promises with no `waitUntil`; loss rate unknown (CHAT-15) | Wrap persistence in `ctx.waitUntil(...)` or await it inside the stream's `execute()`; count requested versus persisted rows per `requestId` | `src/routes/api/chat.ts` |
| Voice: STT transcription latency and accuracy (Arabic and English) and TTS time to first audio | Production domains blocked from the sandbox and provider keys unavailable, so neither route could be called; the four route files record no timing, and the repo holds no labelled Arabic/English audio set (UX baseline: transcription latency not measured) | Server timing around the provider call in both routes (request received → provider response → first audio byte), logged with `requestId`; a client mark from capture stop to transcript and from TTS request to first playback; a small labelled Arabic and English audio set scored for word error rate | `src/routes/api/sadeed/transcribe.ts`, `src/routes/api/sadeed/tts.ts` and their `/api/public/` fallbacks; voice client in `SadeedCopilot.tsx` |
