---

## 7. Earlier hypotheses

Each in-scope hypothesis from the earlier pass, plus new ones the auditors added (marked "(new)"), was re-tested by the area auditors; the table records confirmed / partly / refuted with consolidated evidence, and hypotheses judged out of scope are not listed. Notable results: the RAG embedding call does have a 15 s × 3 deadline; of the new hypotheses, the client's 30 s timeout does not bound chat and chat history is never persisted.

| Area | Hypothesis | Verdict | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| CHAT | /api/chat uses Gemini only via buffered generateText and emits a single text-delta (deliberate safe-output gateway), so there is no real token streaming | confirmed | chat.ts:256 (Gemini-only policy), 795-810 generateText (buffered), 765-768 comment 'Buffer the FULL model turn … Option A', 965-984 one text-start, one text-delta and one text-end. Fixed paths do the same (417-430, 281-292). The Response is constructed only after generation, so even headers wait. |
| CHAT | 8-9 sequential awaits/round-trips happen before the model call | partly | Authenticated: about 11 sequential awaits before generateText (body, getUser, org_members, rate, quota global, quota identity, import instant-data, import ctx+rag, [snapshot ‖ RAG], import tools, adaptive prefs, convertToModelMessages). 7 of them are network stages in the base case (chat.ts:331, 345, 380, 390→ai-quota ×2, 493-505, 725), and 10 with RAG (embed → RPCs → memory update inside the batch) plus the HCIS read (574). Guests: 3 network stages (rate + 2 quota). So '8-9' is roughly right for authenticated turns with RAG off, and wrong for guests. |
| CHAT | gatherRagContext is vector-only (768 dims, RPCs aria_match_*), gated by SADEED_GEMINI_RAG_READY; the RAG embedding call has no deadline; sanitize is not applied to retrieved context; rateLimitRag exists but is unused | partly | Confirmed: vector-only over aria_match_memory/knowledge/documents (rag.server.ts:102-106), 768 dims (embeddings.server.ts:9), gate isGeminiRagReady plus orgId (rag.server.ts:78-79, 96). rateLimitRag has no importer outside src/lib/sadeed/rag/ (grep). sanitizeRetrievedContent and redactPii are not applied. Refuted: the embedding call does have a deadline, httpJson timeoutMs 15_000 with retries 2 (gemini-embeddings.server.ts; http.server.ts:63-104), so about 46s worst case; it is just not tied to request.signal or a stage budget. Partly refuted on sanitize: chat applies untrustedPromptData (escapes < > \0, 48k cap) and UNTRUSTED labels (chat.ts:109-113, 514-551). |
| CHAT | firstTokenAt is always null (no TTFT telemetry) | confirmed | chat.ts:718 `const firstTokenAt: number \| null = null;` and 890 `first_token_ms: firstTokenAt ? … : null`. In addition, startAt (717) is set after the context stages, so latency_ms leaves out pre-model time. rag_query_log has 0 rows (lead). |
| CHAT | aria_match_documents filters by user only (not org) | confirmed | supabase/migrations/20260625235058_d23d69ee-1669-4b08-a09c-171cf45056f1.sql:59-72 `WHERE c.user_id = _user_id`, with no org parameter. The caller passes only _user_id (rag.server.ts:105). Production body references aria_document_chunks and fails with 42P01 (lead fact). The rename preflight (supabase/preflight/finn_rename_verify_and_rollback.sql:43-45, 83-86) checked that the functions exist, not their bodies. |
| CHAT | src/lib/sadeed/file-inspection.server.ts is unused | confirmed | Path correction: the file is src/lib/security/file-inspection.server.ts (src/lib/sadeed/file-inspection.server.ts does not exist). inspectSadeedAttachment is imported only by src/lib/security/file-inspection.server.test.ts (grep). Chat attachments go to Gemini after the guard's MIME, size and data-URL checks only (-chat-guard.ts:111-147), with no magic-byte inspection. |
| CHAT | Guests share a single rate-limit bucket | confirmed | chat.ts:91-100 clientIp returns 'shared' unless TRUST_PROXY_HEADERS=true (set nowhere in repo docs or config). Rate key chat:ip:shared at 12 per 300s (378-381). Quota identity ip:shared at 120 per day (392; ai-quota.server.ts LIMITS.chat). Production env value not measured, so this holds by default and is conditional on that env var. |
| CHAT | (new) The client's 30s hard timeout in sadeedAuthedFetch bounds chat requests | refuted | sadeed-service.ts:46,59 `signal: init.signal ?? controller.signal`. AI SDK 6.0.282 always passes `signal: abortSignal` (node_modules/ai/dist/index.mjs:14208), created per request at 14537/14619, so the timer's controller is never attached. The timeout still works only for pingChat. |
| CHAT | (new) Chat history persists to threads/messages from the copilot | refuted | useChat id 'sadeed-singleton' (sadeed-provider.tsx:52) is sent as body.id (sadeed-service.ts:122). The guard keeps threadId only if it is a UUID (-chat-guard.ts:209-215), so threadId is undefined and the block at chat.ts:902-945 never runs. |
| INGEST | /api/chat uses Gemini only via buffered generateText and emits a single text-delta (no real token streaming) | confirmed | chat.ts:795-811 await generateText(...) buffers the full turn. chat.ts:965-970 writes one text-delta of safeDisplayText, with header X-SADEED-Reply-Source 'safe-gateway' (989). The comment at 765-768 calls this deliberate. |
| INGEST | gatherRagContext is vector-only (768, aria_match_*), gated by SADEED_GEMINI_RAG_READY; RAG embedding has no deadline; sanitize not applied; rateLimitRag unused | partly | Confirmed: vector-only RPCs aria_match_memory/knowledge/documents (rag.server.ts:102-106), 768 dims (embeddings.server.ts:9), env gate (rag.server.ts:78-96). Confirmed: sanitizeRetrievedContent is not applied (only untrustedPromptData replaces '<>', chat.ts:109-113). Confirmed: rateLimitRag is defined at rag/sanitize.server.ts:70 with no callers. Refuted in part: each embedding attempt has a 15s timeout with 2 retries (gemini-embeddings.server.ts:98-109). However, there is no overall deadline, and Retry-After is unbounded (http.server.ts:83-85). |
| INGEST | firstTokenAt is always null (no TTFT telemetry) | confirmed | chat.ts:718 declares 'const firstTokenAt: number \| null = null' and it is never reassigned. Line 890 therefore always persists first_token_ms null. |
| INGEST | aria_match_documents filters by user only (not org) | confirmed | Migration 20260625235058:59-70 has 'WHERE c.user_id = _user_id AND c.embedding IS NOT NULL', with no org and no status filter. The call site rag.server.ts:105 passes only _user_id although orgId is available (82-96). The prod body's WHERE clause was not re-measured by me; per the lead, the prod body is broken (42P01). |
| INGEST | kb-embed produces 768 dims while some column is vector(1536) | partly | kb-embed.server.ts reuses EMBEDDING_DIMS=768. The prod type of sec_knowledge_articles.embedding was not measured. finn_document_chunks.embedding is vector(768) in prod, which matches what ingestion writes. |

The comment at chat.ts:765-768 states 'No raw model token is ever piped'. |

Refuted: the embedding does have a deadline, 15 s per attempt with retries 2 (gemini-embeddings.server.ts:107-108; http.server.ts:76-98), but it is not bound to request.signal and has no tight budget. |

| QA | /api/chat uses Gemini only via buffered generateText and emits a single text-delta (a 'safe-output gateway'), so there is no real token streaming | confirmed | chat.ts:256-259 uses a single Google key, and :396 createGoogleGeminiProvider. :795 `await generateText(...)`, then :965-970 createUIMessageStream writes text-start, one text-delta(safeDisplayText), text-end. The comment at :765-768 reads 'No raw model token is ever piped to the browser (Option A)'. The fixed paths at :285 and :422 are also single deltas. |
| QA | 8–9 sequential awaits or round-trips happen before the model call | partly | There are 11 unconditional awaits before generateText (295, 331, 345, 380, 390, 462, 489, 493, 695, 725, 798) plus 2 conditional (574 HCIS, 650 deep council). Strictly sequential network round-trips: 7 for an authenticated default turn (getUser, org_members, rate RPC, 2 quota RPCs at ai-quota.server.ts:182 and :204, [snapshot∥rag], prefs), 8 with the RAG flag on, 9 with an HCIS query. A guest turn has 3 RPCs plus the snapshot. |
| QA | gatherRagContext is vector-only (768 dims, aria_match_* RPCs), gated by SADEED_GEMINI_RAG_READY; the RAG embedding call has no deadline; sanitize is not applied to retrieved context; rateLimitRag exists but is unused | partly | Confirmed: rag.server.ts:79 checks the env flag, :96 fails closed without orgId or the flag, :98 calls embedText (768, embeddings.server.ts:9), and :102-106 call 3 aria_match_* RPCs with no lexical leg. No sanitize call appears in rag.server.ts; chat.ts wraps the result only with untrustedPromptData (:520, :550). rateLimitRag is defined at sanitize.server.ts:70 and referenced nowhere else (grep). Refuted: 'no deadline'. embedGeminiText uses httpJson with timeoutMs 15_000 and retries 2 (gemini-embeddings.server.ts:98-108), although it is not tied to request.signal. |
| QA | firstTokenAt is always null (no TTFT telemetry) | confirmed | chat.ts:718 `const firstTokenAt: number \| null = null;` is never reassigned (it is a const), so :890 always writes first_token_ms null. On the client, voice-only console timing (SadeedCopilot.tsx:1002-1021, :1118) treats the buffered reply's arrival as the 'first token'. |
| QA | src/lib/sadeed/file-inspection.server.ts is unused | partly | That path does not exist. The real module is src/lib/security/file-inspection.server.ts, and it is imported only by src/lib/security/file-inspection.server.test.ts (grep for 'file-inspection.server\|inspectFile\|security/file-inspection' outside tests matches nothing). So it is unused in production code. |
| QA | Guests share a single rate-limit bucket | partly | chat.ts:91-92: clientIp() returns 'shared' unless TRUST_PROXY_HEADERS === 'true', so the rate key is 'chat:ip:shared' (12 requests per 300 s, chat.ts:378-381) and the quota identity is 'ip:shared' (:392). The production value of TTRUST_PROXY_HEADERS was not measured. When it is set, the first x-forwarded-for entry is preferred over cf-connecting-ip (:93-97). |

## 8. Phase 02 benchmark

Classifications compare documented vendor capability on one dimension with SADEED's evidenced behaviour at `ea9a947`. **Exceeds (measured)** requires a comparative measurement; none exists, so no dimension is classified that way. OpenAI and Google cells come from search summaries, mirrors or labelled secondary sources (lower confidence, §1.5). These are not whole-product comparisons.

| Dimension | ChatGPT | Gemini | Claude | SADEED today (evidenced) | Classification |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Time to first token / streaming | API streams over SSE (`stream=true`); the Responses API has typed streaming events. No TTFT figures published. | API offers `streamGenerateContent` beside `generateContent`; Firebase presents streaming as showing partial output sooner. No latency figures published. | Messages API `stream: true` (SSE: message_start, content-block deltas, ping, error events). No TTFT figures published. | Fully buffered: one `text-delta` after generation, finalize and seal; headers wait for the whole turn (CHAT-02). TTFT not measured; `first_token_ms` always null (QA-15). 7 sequential round trips before the model (QA-21). | Below reference |
| 2. Cancellation / stop | API cancel works only for background responses still queued or in progress; UI stop not verified. | No official cancel endpoint or app stop documentation found (not verified). | No cancel endpoint documented for a streaming message; docs describe recovering an interrupted stream; voice mode has Stop and speech interruption. | Client stop exists; server stages and side effects are not reliably cancelled and stopped turns are persisted (CHAT-03); no turn deadline (CHAT-10). | Not comparable / not verified |
| 3. Context and continuity | Per-plan context windows unconfirmed (third-party figures conflict). Memory: saved memories plus chat-history reference; Temporary Chats excluded. | 32K tokens without a plan, 1M on AI Pro/Ultra (secondary, Sept 2025). Notebooks in Gemini (Apr 2026) hold chats, instructions and files. | API: 1M-token context on current models, compaction (beta), context editing. Apps: topic memory, per-project memory, past-chat search on paid plans. | Client sends 20 messages, server keeps 12, no summary of dropped turns (CHAT-11). Reload loses the conversation; threads never written (CHAT-13). Memory depends on an unconfirmed flag and an RPC reading a renamed table (CHAT-12). | Below reference |
| 4. Knowledge retrieval / grounded answers | Project files (limits differ between FAQ versions); API file search returns `file_citation` annotations. | Notebooks sync with NotebookLM; API grounding with Google Search returns `groundingMetadata`. File Search tool not covered. | Project knowledge switches to RAG automatically near the context limit ("up to 10x"); API citations on document and search-result blocks. | 8 ready documents / 18 chunks cannot reach answers: `aria_match_documents` fails 42P01 (RAG-01, INGEST-34); library search fails (RAG-03). Of `finn_knowledge`, only the HCIS reference rows (source `'hcis/%'`) reach answers, by a direct read (`chat.ts:566-617`; RAG-30); its other rows do not. | Below reference |
| 5. File uploads: formats and limits | 512MB per file; text/docs 2M tokens each; images 20MB; spreadsheets about 50MB; 80 files per 3 h (FAQ via search summary). | App: up to 10 files per prompt; video 2GB, other files 100MB. API Files: 2GB per file, 20GB per project, 48 h retention. | Apps: PDF, DOCX, CSV, TXT, HTML, ODT, RTF, EPUB, JSON, XLSX; JPEG/PNG/GIF/WebP; 500MB per file, 20 files per chat (another article says 30MB). | Chat: PDF, PNG/JPEG/WEBP, TXT, CSV, MD, JSON; at most 5 files, 10 MiB each, 20 MiB total on the server versus 8/16 MB on the client (CHAT-26, INGEST-19). KU: PDF, DOCX, text-like; no byte cap; buckets without limits. No PPTX, OCR or DOCX in chat (INGEST-13, INGEST-02, INGEST-03); XLSX only on the legacy page (INGEST-10); no magic-byte check (INGEST-18). | Below reference |
| 6. Arabic support | No official language list retrieved; Arabic not verified. | Gemini Live expanded to 40+ languages (Oct 2024); Arabic not confirmed in retrieved snippets. | Arabic at 97.2% (Sonnet 4.5, deprecated) and 92.5% (Haiku 4.5) of English on translated MMLU; no figures for current models; not on the dictation or display-language lists. | Arabic-first product; correct Arabic shaping in PDF (UX-27, UX-30). But errors reach users in English or as codes (CHAT-07, UX-05), on-screen bidi misorders mixed text (UI-04), KU is English-first (UX-45, UI-25). Arabic/English answer parity untested end to end (QA-03); quality not measured. | Not comparable / not verified |
| 7. Long-document handling | 2M tokens per file at ingest; usable amount depends on plan context (unconfirmed). No official page limit found. | API: PDFs to 1,000 pages at 258 tokens per page; app 1M context on AI Pro/Ultra (secondary). | 1M-token context; up to 600 PDF pages or images per request; apps: PDFs to 1,000 pages (first 100 visual); docs warn about "context rot". | 250,000-char text cap (INGEST-19); a max-size document makes 179–249 embedding calls inside one request (INGEST-21); page numbers lost (INGEST-27); the legacy path drops at least 751,322 of 2,000,000 chars while marking ready (INGEST-30); no OCR (INGEST-02). | Below reference |

Deletes −, ≥, ≤, €, arrows, ✓/✗ (UX-22); title overflow (UX-24); tables lose columns (UX-28); no sources (UX-21); PDF only; iOS not verified (UX-32). | Below reference |

**Tally:** Below reference 9 (dimensions 1, 3, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11, 12); Not comparable / not verified 4 (2, 6, 8, 13); Comparable (verified) 0; Exceeds (measured) 0.

### Five highest-impact improvements (ranked)

SEC-01 is not on this list because it is a release precondition (WS-0), not an improvement.

1. **Repair retrieval on `finn_*`, org-scoped and logged, with honest status** — RAG-01, INGEST-34, CHAT-14, RAG-03, RAG-08, RAG-21, INGEST-35, UX-37 (WS-1, WS-4). *Rationale:* dimensions 4, 7 and 9 cannot even be measured while the only reader of the uploaded corpus fails with 42P01. The corpus and the ingestion pipeline already exist, so this unlocks the most capability per change. The flag stays off until the measured gate (§9.6).
2. **Early response, then sentence streaming behind a flag, with TTFT telemetry first** — CHAT-02, CHAT-18, QA-14, QA-15, QA-04, QA-21, CHAT-21 (WS-8, WS-9). *Rationale:* dimension 1 is the owner's main complaint (speed). Today headers wait for the whole turn and nothing records first-token time, so control ④ (before/after measurement) cannot be met from in-product telemetry until WS-8; scripted preview fetches are the interim measurement.
3. **Bounded turns and honest recovery** — CHAT-10, CHAT-04, CHAT-07, UX-05, CHAT-09, CHAT-08 (WS-2). *Rationale:* dimensions 2 and 11. A hang currently has no upper bound, and error text breaks the Arabic-first rule and saved-method rule 3.
4. **Truthful gateway and visible, validated citations in chat and PDF** — CHAT-17, CHAT-05, INGEST-37, RAG-11, RAG-12, UI-07, UX-21, INGEST-27 (WS-3, WS-10). *Rationale:* dimensions 9 and 10. Today the gateway's `RE_PHONE` rule runs on every answer and rewrites ISO dates, decimal series and long reference numbers wherever they appear; invented tags can pass, and no source is ever shown or exported.
5. **Server-side extraction breadth and integrity** — INGEST-02, INGEST-03, INGEST-13, INGEST-10, INGEST-21, INGEST-18, INGEST-30 (WS-5, WS-11). *Rationale:* dimensions 5 and 7. Scanned PDFs, PPTX and DOCX-in-chat are impossible; the legacy path silently truncates; nothing validates file bytes.

### Research caveats

- **Access:** OpenAI and Google official pages could not be fetched (DNS failure, proxy 403); their cells rest on search summaries, a regional mirror, or labelled third-party sources.
- **Not published by any vendor in the pages read:** TTFT or latency figures, uptime SLAs, accessibility conformance statements. Third-party uptime tracker figures were deliberately left out.
- **Conflicting figures:**
  - ChatGPT per-plan context windows conflict across third-party sources, and the official pricing page was unreachable.
  - Two versions of the ChatGPT project file-count limits exist; which one is live was not confirmed.
  - Claude upload size: 500MB per file in chat (Upload Files article, updated 2026-07-23) versus 30MB per file ("Create and edit files" article).
  - Gemini: the 5-minute total video length comes from a university mirror only; the API inline request limit is reported as 20MB, 50MB (PDF) or 100MB; the AI Plus 128K figure is third-party only; the app context figures come from Sept 2025 secondary reproductions and may be outdated.
- **Arabic:** not confirmed in any official voice or dictation language list for the three platforms; for Claude it is explicitly absent from the dictation and display-language lists. Claude's Arabic text figures apply to Sonnet 4.5 (deprecated) and Haiku 4.5 only.
- **Model names** are as written in vendor docs read on 2026-10-12. Firebase states that Gemini 2.5 models shut down in October 2026.
- **Not covered** within the research budget, and marked "not verified" rather than filled in: OpenAI transcription API limits, the ChatGPT Voice Mode FAQ, ChatGPT and Gemini UI "stop" docs, the Gemini API File Search tool, Gemini data residency and client-side encryption, Gemini Canvas/Deep Research export, ChatGPT deep research export.

## 9. Owner decisions & conflicts requiring approval

Fifteen items, deduplicated from the auditors' lists. Each gives the options and a recommended option. Every item that touches the answer path ("مسار الإجابة") falls under the owner's 2026-10-11 exception and needs all four controls together: ① a small step; ② tests that cover it; ③ clearance from `nexios-qa-guardian`; ④ a before/after measurement written in the commit message. Any item that changes the database follows the CLAUDE.md order: push → apply the migration → verify under `set local role anon` / `authenticated` → Publish. No Publish happens before the migration.

### 9.1 SEC-01 hotfix now (release-blocking), and the bundled maintenance export

**Facts.** The git-tracked `src/lib/exec/maintenance-report.data.json` has 249 rows, all with `created_by` (20 distinct identifiers: 13 numeric of 5–6 digits, 7 shaped as 2 letters + 10 digits), tracked since 2026-09-02. `buildNotes` writes `أنشأه: <created_by>` into every row's notes. Those notes reach anonymous visitors through the guest chat context and through the `getWorkOrders` fallback (`sec.functions.ts:530-548`, `optionalSupabaseAuth` + `resilient`). This conflicts with CLAUDE.md «الصيانة» §2 (identity masked for non-members), with the owner decision of 2026-10-07 ② (hide employee numbers), and with «البيانات الحقيقية لا تدخل git». `maintenance-report.contract.test.ts:72-73` (expects all 249 notes to contain `أنشأه:`) and `projects-maintenance-recall.test.ts:112` lock the current behaviour.

**Options.**

- **A — Code hotfix now (WS-0).** Strip `created_by` from notes for any anonymous or non-member audience, both in the recall block (`buildRecallBlock` / `formatWorkOrderRecord`, including the notes match field) and in the `sec.functions` fallback. Update the two locking tests and add the SEC-01 contract test.
- **B — A, plus a redacted copy of the export going forward.** Replace the tracked JSON with a copy without `created_by`, so new clones and bundles never carry it.
- **C — A, plus removal of the bundled export.** Serve these reads from `maint_orders_v`, so masking happens in the database. This is a larger change to the Layer-4 `resilient()` fallback design and fits the page-by-page simulation-removal decision of 2026-10-07.
- **Not available: purging the file from git history.** CLAUDE.md forbids history rewrite and force push. **The 20 identifiers therefore stay in git history** (since 2026-09-02) and stay readable to anyone with repository read access. The owner should treat them as already disclosed to repository readers.

**Recommended:** A immediately (release-blocking: no other workstream ships first), with B in the same workstream; C later, under the page-by-page decision.

### 9.2 Deployment authority: PHASE 15.2 "deploy only with explicit authorization" vs the direct-to-main policy (2026-08-01)

**Facts.** CLAUDE.md's Mandatory Direct-to-Main Delivery Policy pushes every task that passes the gate straight to `origin/main`, then Publishes and verifies, with no PR and no new approval request. It declares itself governing over any conflicting instruction. The new PHASE 15.2 requires explicit authorization before deploying. Both cannot hold for the same change.

**Options.**

- **A —** PHASE 15.2 governs this remediation programme: an explicit "go" before every push and Publish.
- **B — Hybrid.** One explicit approval per workstream; inside an approved workstream the direct-to-main chain runs unchanged (push → Publish → production check, no PR). Items CLAUDE.md already keeps behind explicit approval (data deletion, dropping tables or columns, destructive migrations, auth or permission changes, publishing secrets, billing changes, disabling a production integration, sensitive security settings) still need item-level approval. Non-destructive migrations also need owner approval under the PHASE 15 routing rule (§11 c: "applied only with owner approval").
- **C —** The direct-to-main policy stays unchanged for code; PHASE 15.2 applies to DB only.

**Recommended: B.** The owner should record which instruction prevails in CLAUDE.md, because without that record the two texts contradict each other. Article 35 (two writers: `git fetch` and read new commits before every push) applies under every option.

### 9.3 Production database changes that need approval

All changes are non-destructive unless noted. Each one, including the bucket limits in e6, ships as an idempotent SQL migration plus a rollback file in `supabase/migrations/` (for e6 the rollback restores the prior values, today NULL), applied only after approval.

| # | Change | IDs | Options | Recommended |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| e1 | Re-point the `aria_match_documents` / `_knowledge` / `_memory` bodies to `finn_document_chunks` + `finn_documents`, `finn_knowledge` and `finn_memory`; add a required `_org_id`, filter `status = 'ready'`, fail closed when `_org_id` is null | RAG-01, RAG-08, SEC-07, INGEST-34, CHAT-12 | (i) replace the bodies in place with the same signatures (org scoping left to RLS); (ii) new org-scoped functions with a required `_org_id`, callers switched, old functions dropped after verification (the drop needs its own approval) | (ii). Adding a parameter changes the signature anyway, and an explicit org predicate is what RAG-08 asks for. Answer path: four controls. |
| e2 | `rag_gaps` is absent while `rag_record_gap_for_org` inserts into it; the chat tool calls it inside the request path | RAG-04, RAG-05, SEC-10 | (i) create `rag_gaps` plus a scheduled capture job, with EXECUTE revoked from anon/authenticated (Article 26); (ii) drop the RPC; (iii) no DB change now, remove the request-path call in code | (iii) now, because it fixes the `TypeError` with no DB risk. Then (i) if a scheduled capture job is designed in the same change (Article 26); otherwise (ii). |
| e3 | ANN index: `ivfflat lists=100` over 18 rows with default probes 1 can return fewer than k rows | RAG-24, INGEST-33 | (i) drop the ivfflat (exact scan at this size); (ii) replace with HNSW; (iii) keep it and `SET LOCAL ivfflat.probes` in the RPC | (i) until the corpus reaches the thousands, then (ii). |
| e4 | Lexical arm: `tsvector` over `normalize_ar(content)` with a GIN index and a trigram index on `finn_document_chunks`; an org-required `finn_hybrid_search` RPC reusing the RRF pattern of `sec_kb_hybrid_search` | RAG-06, RAG-20, INGEST-33 | (i) with e1; (ii) after e1 is verified, before the RAG gate is measured | (ii), so the gate (§9.6) measures the final retrieval. |
| e5 | Ingest integrity: a partial unique index on `(org_id, user_id, revision_hash) WHERE status IN ('pending','processing','ready')`; a UNIQUE `(document_id, chunk_index)` if production lacks one, after a duplicate check | INGEST-24, SEC-26, INGEST-25 | approve / defer | Approve. Run the read-only `pg_indexes` check first (INGEST-25 is P1 UNVERIFIED). |
| e6 | `file_size_limit` and `allowed_mime_types` on `uploads`, `aria-documents`, `aria-attachments`, `aria-attachments-quarantine` | SEC-13, INGEST-18, INGEST-19, QA-06 | values per bucket | Approve. Limits come from the single advertised limit chosen in §9.14; the MIME list comes from the supported formats in §4. |
| e7 | `sec_kb_hybrid_search`: anon EXECUTE in production; the function predicate lets a NULL `_org_id` match published articles from every org, subject only to RLS, which was not measured in production (RAG-09) | RAG-09, SEC-09 | (i) published articles are intentionally public: document that; (ii) require `_org_id` (raise on null), revoke anon EXECUTE, add an org parameter to `sec_kb_search` | (ii), unless the owner states that public, cross-org reading is intended. This is a permission change, so it needs explicit approval. |
| e8 | Telemetry columns on `finn_interactions`: `request_id`, `total_ms`, `pre_model_ms` (optionally per-stage); optional `processing_ms` on `finn_documents` | QA-13, QA-14, CHAT-18, QA-24 | approve / logs only | Approve after the WS-8 log lines exist and a retention policy is set (§9.15). |

### 9.4 Canonical retrieval path, embedding dimension, governing text, orphans

**Facts.** The runtime constitution (`src/lib/sadeed/constitution.ts:46`, Article 31) mandates `retrieveWithEvidenceGate` at 1536 dims, logging, sanitizing and `rateLimitRag` for every retrieval. The 2026-10-11 decision chose to evolve the `finn_*` tables, whose chunks are `vector(768)`. The two constitutions number their articles differently: the runtime file uses 25 for jurisdiction, 31 for the RAG protocol and 32 for RESTRICTIVE RLS; `SADEED_CONSTITUTION.md` uses 25 for the poisoning barrier, 26 for read-side capture and 27 for numeric honesty. Orphans target the unapplied `20260728*` schema: `rag_measure.sql`, the `rag-retrieve` edge function behind `/rag-search`, `rag.propose_candidate`, `rag_record_gap_for_org`, the Article 25 trigger, and the CI rag-preflight.

**Options.**

- **A —** `finn_*` at 768 dims is canonical for uploads. Amend runtime Article 31 so the governed protections (injection screen, sanitize/PII redaction, `rag_query_log`, `rateLimitRag`) wrap the finn RPC. `sec_kb_hybrid_search` stays for reference articles. The 768-d `sec_kb_search` path is retired once RAG-07 is measured.
- **B —** Move all retrieval to 1536-d `retrieveWithEvidenceGate`: add a 1536 column to chunks and re-embed the corpus. This needs a column migration and re-embedding.
- **C —** Keep the parallel stacks.

**Recommended: A.** It is consistent with the 2026-10-11 decision. Declare `SADEED_CONSTITUTION.md` numbering canonical (CLAUDE.md names it the supreme reference) and map the runtime articles to it. For each orphan: port the measurement harness to the finn RPC; retire `rag-retrieve`, `/rag-search` and `rag.propose_candidate`; retarget the CI preflight (§9.15). The "Amended articles" register entry is the owner's.

### 9.5 User uploads as evidence, and their scope (Article 25 poisoning barrier; runtime Article 32 isolation)

**Facts.** Once e1 lands, `gatherRagContext` turns any uploaded chunk into a `[DOC:]` citation that satisfies the regulatory evidence gate. Upload text is extracted on the client and is not bound to the stored file (no server re-extraction, no stored file hash; the stored `revision_hash` is a SHA-256 of the extracted text, used for dedupe), so a fabricated upload would become citable evidence (INGEST-42). There are 0 official `finn_documents`. The current RPC is user-only, while the runtime constitution's Article 32 (`constitution.ts`, a different numbering from `SADEED_CONSTITUTION.md`, see §9.4) requires org-scoped retrieval.

**Options.**

- **A —** Uploads are retrievable as context but not citable for regulatory claims until verified (same-org grounding or admin approval). Retrieval is org-scoped with an explicit predicate and a membership check. Add the Article 25 promotion trigger on `finn_documents.is_official` before any verified flag becomes writable (RAG-26). Add server-side extraction and a stored file sha256 later (WS-11).
- **B —** Uploads are citable immediately.
- **C —** Uploads stay private per user, not shared within the org.

**Recommended: A.** Whether documents are shared org-wide or private per user is a product choice. The default here is org-wide, matching runtime Article 32 and the `tenant_isolation` policy already in production.

### 9.6 Enabling `SADEED_GEMINI_RAG_READY` only after the measured gate

**Facts.**

- The production value of the flag was not measured; it is readable as `rag_ready` from `GET /api/chat/health`. The runbook keeps it false.
- Gate: at least 30 measured questions, recall@5 ≥ 0.85, citation precision ≥ 0.95, on a documented corpus. The thresholds in `src/lib/rag/tuning.ts:85-88` match.
- CLAUDE.md says the anchor has 21 items (so the gate is structurally closed). The code holds 41 anchors under an owner growth decision dated 2026-08-05 (`evaluation.ts:37-48`); whether migration `20260805120000` is applied in production was not measured.
- The current harness measures the unapplied `rag_hybrid_search`. Its "citationAccuracy" is hit@1 rank, not answer-citation precision, and its probes are verbatim titles.

**Options.**

- **A —** The flag stays false until the gate passes on the §9.4 path, using paraphrased probes and true answer-citation precision, with results recorded in an eval ledger.
- **B —** Enable after an e1 smoke test.

**Recommended: A.** The owner should also restate the anchor count (21 or 41) so that CLAUDE.md and the code agree. Until then no recall or precision number may be quoted (Article 27).

### 9.7 Streaming behind a flag (the 2026-10-11 decision)

**Facts.** The flag does not exist. Whole-text gateway rules (exec-claim, evidence, exfiltration) assume the full text, so once text is already on screen a later rule can fail. Control ④ cannot be met from in-product telemetry until WS-8 ships; scripted preview fetches are the interim measurement. Article 24(b)'s 250 ms "first token → sentence 1" ceiling is computed against a "first token" that is today the whole reply.

**Options.**

- **Step 1 (no gateway change):** return the stream response at once, move the work into `execute()`, and emit status parts and a heartbeat while the final text still passes the gateway as one delta.
- **Step 2, A:** under a flag (e.g. `SADEED_SENTENCE_STREAM`, default off), each sentence passes per-sentence checks before display, and a final whole-text pass may retract and replace what was shown.
- **Step 2, B:** hold back only the first sentence until it passes.
- **C:** stay buffered.

**Recommended:** approve Step 1 now; it does not wait for WS-8, and its control ④ is satisfied by scripted preview fetches (TTFB and total over N runs) recorded in the commit message. Adopt Step 2-A, enabled only after WS-8 telemetry provides the before/after numbers. Define Article 24(b)'s "first token" as the first displayed text delta.

### 9.8 Write actions inside chat: confirm before execution, or truthful receipts after

**Facts.** CLAUDE.md requires every write or emergency action to sit behind a human confirmation gate («أي إجراء كتابة أو طوارئ خلف بوابة تأكيد بشري»). Yet chat writer tools (`sec_create_incident`, `sec_decide_permit`, `send_executive_email`/`sms`, `create_task`, …) execute inside `generateText`. The gateway then tells the user the action "requires approval" (CHAT-17), and regenerate can repeat it (CHAT-05).

**Options.**

- **A —** A confirmation gate before execution: a writer tool returns a proposal, the user confirms in the UI, and execution returns a receipt.
- **B —** Keep direct execution, pass same-turn receipts to the gateway, and dedupe by turn id.
- **C (stopgap) —** Hide "regenerate" after a writer ran.

**Recommended: A**, consistent with CLAUDE.md. Receipts and the turn-id key apply under every option, and C ships immediately in WS-3. A turn-id ledger table, if chosen, is a §9.3-style DB change.

### 9.9 Resilience layers 1 and 2

**Facts.** Layer 1 ("never 503") conflicts with the fail-closed rate limit and quota: an RPC outage returns 503 even for no-model answers, and `chat-latency.contract.test.ts` deliberately limits the guard to the no-key branch (CHAT-09). Layer 2 ("cool the failed provider, next attempt goes to another configured provider") conflicts with the Gemini-only policy. No cooldown exists, yet `SadeedCopilot.tsx:1859-1861` claims one (CHAT-08).

**Options.**

- **Layer 1:**
  - **A —** When rate or quota checks are unavailable, still serve the deterministic paths (scope guard, fast recall, instant data) and the local guaranteed reply as a 200 stream. Fail closed only for the paid model call.
  - **B —** Keep 503 and amend CLAUDE.md layer 1.
- **Layer 2:**
  - **A —** A configured secondary Gemini model id plus a cooldown after 429, 5xx or timeout (within the Gemini-only policy).
  - **B —** Amend CLAUDE.md layer 2 and delete the false comment.

**Recommended: A for both.** If layer-2 A is declined, B is required immediately, because documentation must not claim a layer that does not exist.

### 9.10 Guest access policy

**Facts.**

- `TRUST_PROXY_HEADERS` is unset in all repo config and docs, and its production value is unknown. If unset, every guest shares one bucket (12 requests per 5 minutes, 120 per day). If set, a spoofable first `x-forwarded-for` entry wins over `cf-connecting-ip` (CHAT-22, SEC-06, QA-22).
- The attachment charter (§1/§3) says anonymous users may not upload, but the attach button renders for guests and `/api/chat` accepts guest file parts, up to 5 files / 20 MiB per request (INGEST-40).
- `voice-token` lets any signed-in user mint ElevenLabs tokens, while the `hook-auth` comment intends executives (SEC-04).

**Options and recommendations:**

- **Rate-limit identity.**
  - **A —** Trust `cf-connecting-ip` by default; XFF only behind the env var and never first; expose `trust_proxy` in `/api/chat/health`.
  - **B —** Keep the shared bucket.
  - **Recommended: A.**
- **Guest attachments.**
  - **A —** Hide or disable attach for guests until server-side inspection exists (INGEST-18).
  - **B —** Amend the charter.
  - **Recommended: A.**
- **Voice-token minting.**
  - **A —** Require `verifySupabaseExecutive`.
  - **B —** Keep any signed-in user.
  - **Recommended: A**, matching the stated intent.

### 9.11 Theme direction: "permanent light mode" vs Articles 32–34 (dual mode)

**Facts.** A directive mentions a "permanent light-mode direction". Articles 32–34 make both modes a correctness surface: every check runs twice. The boot script defaults to dark (`__root.tsx:279-283`). The chat panel's light mode is broken today (UI-21: title 1.04:1), so a light-only direction would make UI-21 a launch blocker. Related policies:

- **Reduced motion:** the owner retained the `nx-glow-breathe` / `hx-halo-breathe` glow, and as implemented with `!important` it overrides `prefers-reduced-motion` (11 infinite animations measured) (UI-19).
- **Touch targets:** the token layer gives 44 px only on coarse pointers and 40 px on fine pointers (UI-17).
- **Panel identity:** the panel is deliberately "cinematic dark" but uses theme-token text (UI-21, UI-22).

**Options.**

- **A —** Keep dual mode; fix light mode in WS-6.
- **B —** Permanent light: amend Articles 32–34 and change the boot default.
- **C —** Light as the default, with dual mode retained.
- **Panel:**
  - **(a) —** Always dark, with dark tokens scoped to `.sadeed-window`.
  - **(b) —** The panel follows the theme with tokenised surfaces.

**Recommended:**

- **Theme direction:** A, or C if the owner wants a light-first experience. C needs no amendment of the articles, since both modes stay checked.
- **Panel:** (b). Either panel option removes the literal colours that break Article 33.
- **Reduced motion:** the reduced-motion preference wins over the retained glow (WCAG 2.2.2).
- **Touch targets:** keep 44 px coarse / 40 px fine unless the owner requires 44 px for mouse pointers too.

### 9.12 Sky-blue UI accents vs "sky palette for PDF files only"

**Facts.** The owner decision on record limits the sky palette (`#38BDF8 #2563EB #F0F9FF #22D3EE #34D399 #FFFFFF #F8FAFC #0F172A`) to PDF files; the chat PDF implements it (UX-23). CLAUDE.md keeps navy/emerald as the product palette, and Article 33 forbids any colour outside the token layer.

**Options.**

- **A —** Keep sky PDF-only (the status quo).
- **B —** Introduce sky accents in the UI. This amends the PDF-only decision and must be done only through `--tone-*` tokens, checked in both themes.

**Recommended: A.** If B is chosen, record the amendment and implement it through tokens only. WS-6 does not depend on this choice beyond avoiding new literal colours, so §9.12 is not listed among WS-6's dependencies and no workstream waits for it.

### 9.13 Answer-shaping product choices (answer path; four controls)

**Reply mode for typed turns (CHAT-23).**

- **Fact:** every typed turn is sent as `replyMode: 'voice'` (V5 "speak every reply"), which compresses typed reports into 1–3 spoken sentences and conflicts with the system prompt's "do not collapse a requested report" and with PDF export.
- **Options:** (A) separate "speak it" from the voice contract: typed turns send `'text'`, and speech is derived client-side with the existing `toSadeedSpeechText`; (B) keep the current behaviour.
- **Recommended: A.**

**Clock block placement (CHAT-19).**

- **Fact:** code cites "Article 2 (hard)" to put `<SADEED_RUNTIME>` at the very start of every turn. `SADEED_CONSTITUTION.md` Article 2 is "Supremacy", and the constitution has no time clause. The clock at offset 0 prevents reuse of the 42,600-char static prefix.
- **Options:** (A) server-derived every turn, placed at the end; (B) keep it at the start.
- **Recommended: A**, if the owner confirms that "at the start" is not binding.

**Transcript editing before send (UX-07).**

- **Options:** (A) an optional dictation mode that fills the draft without auto-sending, with conversation mode unchanged; (B) no change.
- **Recommended: A.**

**Deep council (CHAT-27).**

- **Options:** (A) an explicit «مراجعة معمّقة» action; (B) document it as API-only.
- **Recommended: B** for now.

### 9.14 Knowledge library and ingestion policy

**Retire the legacy `/sadeed-documents` ingest (INGEST-17, INGEST-30).**

- **Options:** (A) retire it and route uploads through the canonical hook, moving XLSX extraction there; (B) keep it.
- **Recommended: A.** The links in `marketplace.tsx:51,59` and `enterprise-search.functions.ts:164` must follow.

**Delete policy (UX-44, UX-46, UI-26).**

- A confirmation dialog is required by CLAUDE.md in every option.
- **Options:** (A) only the document owner or an org manager may delete; delete the row first, then the object; (B) soft delete; (C) any member.
- **Recommended: A.** Soft delete is optional.

**Deposit wording (UX-17, INGEST-35).**

- **Options:** (A) honest wording now ("PDFs and images are read for this turn and not stored"); (B) server-side deposit of PDFs and images, which stores more user files.
- **Recommended: A now; B in WS-11 if wanted.**

**`file-inspection.server.ts` (QA-07).**

- **Options:** (A) wire it into the chat guard and the ingest path; (B) delete the module and its test.
- **Recommended: A.**

**Single advertised upload limit (UI-25, INGEST-19).**

- **Fact:** the KU page shows "up to 50MB" and a 250k-character hint; chat uses 10/20 MiB on the server and 8/16 MB on the client.
- **Decision:** the owner picks one byte limit, enforced in the client, the server and the bucket (e6).

**`/knowledge-upload` language (UX-45, UI-25).**

- **Options:** (A) localize Arabic-first and remove endpoint names; (B) classify the page as an internal admin tool exempt from the rule.
- **Recommended: A.**

**PDF conventions (UX-25, UX-30).**

- **Timestamp:** UTC today, or Asia/Riyadh (+03). **Recommended: Asia/Riyadh.**
- **Digits:** Arabic-Indic digits converted to Western today, or kept (the font covers both). This is the owner's preference.

**Source-code and audio files (INGEST-16, INGEST-15).**

- **Decision:** state one allowlist for both paths, and whether recorded briefings should become knowledge.

### 9.15 Retention, measurement and documentation

**Retention (SEC-20).**

- **Fact:** no TTL or retention job exists for `finn_interactions`, `messages` or storage objects. The PDPL implies a decision is needed.
- **Recommended:** a written retention policy before telemetry columns (e8) or server-side deposit (§9.14) add more stored data.

**Production latency probe (QA-20).**

- **Fact:** a probe uses provider quota and writes `finn_interactions` and `messages` rows.
- **Options:** (A) approve it with a synthetic tenant and user and the existing `E2E_TEST_*` secrets, `workflow_dispatch` first and nightly later; (B) preview only.
- **Recommended: A.**

**CI rag-preflight (QA-02).**

- **Fact:** it validates the unapplied `20260728*` schema and exits 0 without Postgres.
- **Options:** (A) retarget it to a read-only production schema-drift probe (`to_regclass` / `to_regprocedure`, function bodies free of renamed tables) that fails rather than skips; (B) retire it.
- **Recommended: A.**

**Live isolation tests (QA-09).**

- **Fact:** `rls-isolation.test.ts` is skipped without live credentials.
- **Recommended:** provide staging credentials, or approve a nightly read-only job with two synthetic tenants under `set local role authenticated`.

**Documentation drift.** These are all stale:

- the RAG anchor count in CLAUDE.md;
- the `instant-data-answers.server.ts:27-31` header, which says guests are excluded;
- the `SadeedCopilot.tsx:1861` and `sadeed-service.test.ts:82` references to retry/cooling layers;
- the quarantine flow described in `CHANGELOG.md:48` and `TESTING.md:84-91` (INGEST-39).

**Recommended:** a `nexios-docs-keeper` pass after WS-4.

## 10. Prioritized remediation plan

**Execution order:** the P0 first; then BROKEN rows before new capability; then PARTIAL rows completed before MISSING ones are added. Within that order, workstreams that unblock others come first.

**Effort scale:** S = one small commit; M = several commits in one area; L = multi-area work with DB changes and a staged release.

**Every workstream** passes the gate tsc → eslint → vitest → build → budget → `nexios-qa-guardian` before its commit, and is released by push → Lovable Publish → `production-check.yml` (workflow_dispatch). The release cadence is per §9.2.

### WS-0 — SEC-01 privacy hotfix (release-blocking)

- **Goal:** no creator employee or ID number reaches an anonymous or non-member reader, through chat context or the work-orders fallback.
- **IDs:** SEC-01 (P0), SEC-25.
- **Files / DB objects:**
  - `src/lib/exec/maintenance-report.real.server.ts` (`buildNotes` `:107`, notes `:147`).
  - `src/lib/sadeed-context.server.ts:69` (pass an audience into `buildRecallBlock`).
  - `src/lib/sadeed/projects-maintenance-recall.server.ts` (`:167` match fields, `:215` `formatWorkOrderRecord`).
  - `src/lib/sec.functions.ts:530-548` (`getWorkOrders` fallback).
  - `src/routes/api/chat.ts:522-541` (guest branch passes `'anonymous'`).
  - Tests: `maintenance-report.contract.test.ts:72-73` and `projects-maintenance-recall.test.ts:112` (both updated), plus a new SEC-01 contract test.
  - Per §9.1-B: a redacted `src/lib/exec/maintenance-report.data.json`.
- **Migration + rollback:** none; rollback is `git revert`. No history rewrite.
- **Risk:** low functional risk. Member-facing notes read from `maint_orders_v` are unchanged; with §9.1-B the bundled export loses the creator line for every audience, so `maintenance-report.contract.test.ts:72-73` (which expects all 249 notes to contain `أنشأه:`) must change. Guest context assembly feeds the model, so the four answer-path controls apply.
- **Acceptance test:**
  - `buildProductionDatasetsSnapshot(<question with a WO ref>, ts, 'anonymous').contextBlock` contains no `أنشأه:` and no creator-shaped value (`[A-Z]{2}\d{10}` or the 5–6-digit creator values).
  - The `getWorkOrders` fallback payload for a null user has notes without `created_by`.
  - The commit message records the before/after count of identifier matches in the guest context for a fixed set of WO questions (before > 0, after = 0).
- **Effort:** S.
- **Depends on:** §9.1, §9.2.

### WS-1 — Knowledge pages: truthful status, real error states, safe delete, Arabic-first

- **Goal:** stop the false readiness and deposit claims and the unsafe one-click deletes while retrieval is still broken.
- **IDs:** INGEST-35 (P1), UX-37 (P1), UX-17, UX-42, UI-28, UX-38 and SEC-15 (interim: disable search with an honest Arabic message; map errors to neutral Arabic), UX-44, UI-26, UI-27, INGEST-29, SEC-16, UX-46 (show the owner; gate delete by owner or manager), UX-45, UI-25, INGEST-45, UI-29, UI-30.
- **Files / DB objects:**
  - `src/routes/_authenticated/knowledge-upload.tsx`: `:295` limit text, `:346-385` banner, `:247` / `:402-405` empty-on-error, `:434-442` delete.
  - `src/routes/_authenticated/sadeed-documents.tsx`: delete `:106` / `:318`, search UI.
  - `SadeedCopilot.tsx`: `:2761` attach title, `:1295` default attach prompt.
  - `knowledge-upload.functions.ts:194` (`citation_ready` definition).
  - `documents.functions.ts:68-83` (delete the row first with `.select()`, then the object, checking both errors) and `:207` (neutral error).
- **Migration + rollback:** none.
- **Risk:** low; UI and server functions only. The default attach prompt is user-message copy on the client, not the server answer path.
- **Acceptance test:**
  - The status reads «مُفهرَس — الاسترجاع غير مفعّل» unless a live retrieval probe returns the document.
  - An `isError` branch shows an Arabic error and a retry.
  - An AlertDialog confirmation names the document title; `aria-label="حذف <title>"`; a failed delete shows an Arabic toast.
  - A string-scan test finds no English-first strings or endpoint names.
  - Screenshots in both themes at 390 and 1280 px.
- **Effort:** M.
- **Depends on:** §9.14 (delete policy, single limit). The confirmation dialog can proceed regardless.

### WS-2 — Bounded turns, Arabic errors, correct retry

- **Goal:** every chat turn ends within a known budget with an Arabic outcome, and retries happen only for transient failures.
- **IDs:** CHAT-10 (P1), CHAT-07, UX-05, UX-02 (one Arabic message per capture failure type), UX-19 (restore the draft and files after a pre-send failure), CHAT-04, CHAT-03, SEC-22, CHAT-09 (per §9.9); then CHAT-08 last (new capability, only if §9.9 layer 2 = A).
- **Files / DB objects:**
  - `src/lib/sadeed-core/services/sadeed-service.ts:46,59`: `AbortSignal.any([init.signal, controller.signal])`, with a budget above the server deadline.
  - `SadeedCopilot.tsx`:
    - a pure `toArabicChatError(raw, status)` used at `:2221-2240` that prefers `message_ar`;
    - a retry classifier at `:1865-1885`;
    - voice error notices (UX-05).
  - `src/routes/api/chat.ts`:
    - a turn deadline `AbortSignal.any([request.signal, AbortSignal.timeout(T)])` threaded into `generateText`, the RAG stage and the pre-model RPCs (`.abortSignal()`);
    - Arabic `chatJsonError` text with a separate `code` field;
    - the §9.9 no-503 deterministic paths;
    - skip persistence when the request is aborted.
  - `-chat-guard.ts` messages; `rate-limit.server.ts`, `ai-quota.server.ts`; the embedding HTTP helper takes `request.signal`.
  - CHAT-08: the secondary model id comes from a server env var set by the owner in Lovable.
- **Migration + rollback:** none.
- **Risk:** medium. The server deadline is on the answer path (four controls). A too-small T would cut legitimate multi-step tool turns (`stepCountIs(12)`).
- **Acceptance test:**
  - Fake timers: `sadeedAuthedFetch` with `init.signal` and a never-resolving fetch rejects at the budget.
  - A hanging-model mock produces the Arabic timeout line within T.
  - Each real server error body maps to Arabic text with no `[a-z_]{6,}` code and no auth wording.
  - The classifier does not retry 429/400/413/`ai_disabled`, and does retry `TypeError`/502.
  - When `check_rate_limit` errors, «كم عدد المشاريع» gets a 200 instant answer and `generateText` is not called.
  - CHAT-08: a mocked primary 503 means the next call uses the secondary, and the primary is skipped within the cooldown.
- **Effort:** M.
- **Depends on:** §9.9, §9.2.

### WS-3 — Output gateway truthfulness, attachments as evidence, idempotent writers

- **Goal:** the gateway neither relabels executed actions nor corrupts dates and numbers, rejects invented citations, and counts current-turn attachments and tool results as evidence; writer tools cannot run twice for one turn.
- **IDs:** CHAT-17 (P1), INGEST-37 (P1), RAG-11 (P1), CHAT-05 (P1), INGEST-38, RAG-13.
- **Files / DB objects:**
  - `src/lib/sadeed/output/finalize.ts`:
    - `RE_PHONE` `:174` limited to KSA phone shapes;
    - `RE_EXEC_CLAIM` `:160-161` honouring receipts;
    - `:440-460` every cited tag must be known, and `SEC-xx` codes map to the KB tags supplied for them;
    - `:455` the envelope lists only the cited subset.
  - `src/routes/api/chat.ts`:
    - `:839-848` build receipts from same-request ok tool results; evidence includes tool citations and `[ATT:filename]` for current-turn attachments;
    - `:416-468` skip the instant-data and fast-recall paths when the last message has file parts.
  - `sadeed-tools.server.ts` writers record `(user_id, turn_id, tool)` and return the prior result on a repeat.
  - `sadeed-service.ts` sends one `x-aria-request-id` per user message, reused on regenerate and retry.
  - Stopgap: `SadeedCopilot.tsx:2186-2195` hides regenerate after a writer ran.
- **Migration + rollback:** only if the turn-id ledger is a new table (§9.3 style, owner-approved, with a rollback file). The stopgap needs none.
- **Risk:** medium-high; this is the core answer path (four controls). Relaxing the gate too far could let unbacked claims through, so tests must cover both directions.
- **Acceptance test:**
  - Finalize tests mirroring the measured probes: a known plus an invented tag → the invented tag is removed or the answer refused; a bare `SEC-02` with matching KB evidence → answer.
  - «تم الإرسال» with a same-turn ok `send_executive_email` receipt → `executed`; without the receipt → `approval_required`.
  - `2026-10-12`, `12.5 13.7 14.2` and `4500012345` pass unchanged, while a KSA mobile number is still masked.
  - A mocked model that calls `sec_create_incident` twice with the same turn id produces one insert.
  - A count question with file parts is not routed to instant-data.
  - Before/after in the commit: attachment-answer corruption 3/3 → 0/3; executed-action relabelling 2/3 → 0/3 with receipts.
- **Effort:** M.
- **Depends on:** §9.8, §9.2.

### WS-4 — Retrieval repair on `finn_*` (flag stays off)

- **Goal:** the retrieval RPCs execute in production, org-scoped and logged, with tests that would catch a regression. The flag stays off until §9.6.
- **IDs:** RAG-01, INGEST-34, CHAT-14, RAG-03, UX-38, SEC-15 (P1); RAG-07 (P1 UNVERIFIED: measure the `sec_knowledge_articles.embedding` type, then route `sec_kb_*` retrieval through one embedder of matching dimension); RAG-04, RAG-05, SEC-10, RAG-21, QA-16 (BROKEN P2); RAG-08, SEC-07; RAG-24, INGEST-33 (index, §9.3 e3); RAG-02 (remove the dead branch `chat.ts:544-552`; pass as evidence only the citations actually injected); RAG-28 (retire or repoint the orphan `/rag-search`, §9.4); RAG-29, QA-01 (tests); RAG-23 (stage budget).
- **Files / DB objects:**

  - New `supabase/preflight/finn_match_check.sql`: runs every `aria_match_*` / finn / `sec_kb_*` function under `set local role authenticated` with two orgs (plpgsql and sql bodies resolve only at call time).
  - `src/lib/sadeed/rag.server.ts:82-213`: new RPC with orgId; errors written to `rag_query_log` (not only `console.warn`); a 2–3 s stage budget; `last_used_at` update fire-and-forget.
  - `src/lib/sadeed/documents.functions.ts:187-217`: same RPC, neutral Arabic error.
  - `src/lib/sadeed-tools.server.ts:509-515`: replace `.rpc(...).catch` with a checked `{ error }`, and remove the request-path gap write (§9.3 e2-iii).
  - `src/lib/sadeed/rag/retrieve.server.ts:113-157`: log early returns, check the insert error.

- **Migration + rollback:** yes.
- **Risk:** high (answer path plus production DDL). Mitigations: the flag stays off, new function names, a rollback file, verification under `set local role` before Publish, and the four controls.
- **Acceptance test:**
  - On production under `set local role authenticated` with a member's claims, the new function returns rows with no 42P01.

- **Depends on:** §9.3 (e1–e3), §9.4, §9.5, §9.2. Order: push → migrate → verify → Publish.

### WS-5 — Ingestion integrity and upload validation

- **Goal:** one canonical ingestion pipeline whose `ready` is true and whose inputs are validated by bytes, size and type.
- **IDs:** INGEST-30 (P1), INGEST-25 (P1 UNVERIFIED), QA-07 (BROKEN), INGEST-17, INGEST-10, INGEST-18, UX-16, SEC-13, SEC-14, INGEST-19, INGEST-20, INGEST-22, SEC-02, INGEST-24, SEC-26, INGEST-26, INGEST-32, INGEST-43, INGEST-41, INGEST-44, UX-43, INGEST-23 (processed/total chunk counts on the row; count with `embedding is not null` in SQL), INGEST-39 (wire the scan ledger into the new inspection, or mark the quarantine objects deprecated in the docs), QA-06 (bucket-limit probe test).
- **Files / DB objects:**
  - Retire `ingestSadeedDocument` (`documents.functions.ts:12-167`) and route `/sadeed-documents` uploads to `enqueueKnowledgeIngest`, moving sheet extraction (INGEST-10) into the canonical path so XLSX does not regress.
  - Update the links in `marketplace.tsx:51,59` and `enterprise-search.functions.ts:164`.
  - `knowledge-upload.functions.ts:117-130`: call the pure `knowledge-ingest-core` in-process (or a pinned origin plus `AbortSignal.timeout`).
  - `sadeed-docs-ingest.ts`: mark the row failed before throwing; use `embedGeminiBatch`; add a per-user ingest rate limit and quota; check the tail-delete error.
  - Wire `file-inspection.server.ts` into `-chat-guard.ts` and the ingest path.
  - `knowledge-upload.tsx`: byte cap; title sliced to 300; surface storage failure; retry action.
  - A cron sweeper that fails stale `processing` rows.
  - DB: §9.3 e5 and e6.
- **Migration + rollback:** yes (indexes, bucket configuration).
- **Risk:** medium; ingestion semantics change. Users of the legacy page get the canonical limits (250k chars instead of 2M).
- **Acceptance test:**
  - The read-only `pg_indexes` check is recorded, then UNIQUE `(document_id, chunk_index)` is present.
  - Hook tests with a mocked `supabaseAdmin` and fetch: `onConflict`, tail-delete error, missing secret → row `failed`, partial embed failure.
  - A PNG-declared data URL containing PDF bytes is rejected.
  - Two concurrent identical uploads produce one document.
  - A 2,000,000-char input is rejected at 250k (no silent truncation).
  - A `storage.buckets` probe test asserts `file_size_limit` and `allowed_mime_types` (QA-06).
  - `ready` only after all chunks are embedded, plus a self-retrieval probe (once WS-4 lands).
- **Effort:** L.
- **Depends on:** WS-4 (self-retrieval probe), §9.3 (e5, e6), §9.14.

### WS-6 — PDF fidelity and chat panel accessibility in both themes

- **Goal:** the exported PDF never changes meaning or layout, and the chat panel is readable and keyboard-correct in light and dark.
- **IDs:** UX-22 (P1), UI-21 (P1), UX-24, UI-22, UI-12, UI-13, UI-04, UI-14, UI-11, UX-28, UX-29, UX-25, UX-31 (ActualText/ToUnicode mapping, or document the limitation), UI-19 (per §9.11), UI-05, UI-23, UI-24, UI-32 (header controls into an overflow menu under 480 px).

  - `src/lib/reports/chat-pdf.ts`: `:57-64` substitute before filtering (− → `-`, ≥ → `>=` or «لا يقل عن», ✓/✗ → «نعم»/«لا», € → `EUR`); `:280-293` wrap the title to at most 2 lines below the brand, Asia/Riyadh time; `:95-114` real table columns, verbatim code, ordinals.
  - `SadeedCopilot.tsx`:
    - replace the literal colours at `:373`, `:2331`, `:2354`, `:2398-2400` and `:2653` with `--surface` / `--glass-*` / `--tone-*` tokens;
    - `:2338-2341` becomes `role="dialog" aria-modal="true"` with a focus trap or `inert`;
    - Escape handling (leave voice mode first); focus returns to the launcher;
    - `role="log" aria-live="polite"`;
    - `unicode-bidi: plaintext` on message prose.
  - `styles.css:4650-4671` (reduced motion); `SadeedMuteToggle.tsx:48`.
  - A contract test banning `rgba()` and hex literals in `SadeedCopilot` class strings.
- **Migration + rollback:** none.
- **Risk:** low-medium (visual regressions). The JS budget has about 133 KB headroom, so PDF changes stay in the lazy chunks.
- **Acceptance test:**

  - A 93-character question title wraps to at most 2 lines without overlapping the brand (checked with `pdftoppm`).
  - A table row with an empty cell keeps its columns.
  - Pixel-sampled light-theme header title at least 4.5:1, icons at least 3:1 at 390@2x; dark unchanged.
  - Escape closes the panel in 8/8 runs; Tab stays inside the panel.

- **Depends on:** §9.11, §9.14 (PDF conventions).

### WS-7 — Guest fairness and retrieval-side hardening

- **Goal:** fair per-visitor limits, a guest policy that matches the charter, and retrieved or uploaded text that is screened before the prompt.
- **IDs:** CHAT-22 (P1), SEC-06, QA-22, INGEST-40, SEC-04, CHAT-16, RAG-10, SEC-08, SEC-12, QA-10, RAG-22, RAG-09, SEC-09, SEC-23, SEC-24, SEC-17, SEC-11, CHAT-01 (optional: when `getUser` fails with a network or 5xx error, return a retryable signal other than 503, such as a structured `auth_unavailable` code that the client retries with the bearer, instead of downgrading to the guest path; layer 1 forbids a new 503, §9.9).
- **Files / DB objects:**
  - `chat.ts:91-100 clientIp` (`cf-connecting-ip` first) and `chat.health.ts` (`trust_proxy`).
  - `SadeedCopilot.tsx:2744-2771`: attach hidden for guests.
  - `rag.server.ts`: `safeExcerpt` / `sanitizeRetrievedContent` / `INJECTION_PATTERNS` / `redactPii` on excerpts; `retrievedDocs` passed to `finalize`.
  - `rag/sanitize.server.ts:70 rateLimitRag` wired into both retrieval paths.
  - A streaming byte cap on the chat body.
  - `queryClient.clear()` on sign-out or user change, plus `Cache-Control: no-store` on AI responses.
  - A server-side `storage_path.startsWith(\`${userId}/\`)` check.
  - DB: §9.3 e7; read-only measurement of `relforcerowsecurity` and `pg_policies.permissive`.
- **Migration + rollback:** yes, for e7.
- **Risk:** medium. Sanitizing retrieved context is on the answer path (four controls). Revoking anon EXECUTE breaks direct anon PostgREST calls; the app caller already guards null org.
- **Acceptance test:**
  - Two requests with different `cf-connecting-ip` get different rate keys, and health shows `trust_proxy`.
  - A seeded chunk saying "ignore previous instructions" stays inside the untrusted block and the output does not obey it.
  - Under `set local role anon`, `sec_kb_hybrid_search` is denied.
  - The SEC-11 measurement is recorded.
  - A guest sees no attach control.
- **Effort:** M.
- **Depends on:** §9.10, §9.3 (e7), §9.2.

### WS-9 — Early response, then sentence streaming behind a flag

- **Goal:** the first bytes arrive at once, and visible text arrives before the whole generation finishes, without weakening the gateway.
- **IDs:** CHAT-02 (P1), CHAT-06, QA-04, CHAT-21, QA-21, CHAT-20, CHAT-24, CHAT-19, CHAT-23.
- **Files / DB objects:**
  - `src/routes/api/chat.ts`:
    - Step 1: `createUIMessageStreamResponse` at once, work inside `execute()`, status parts and a heartbeat.
    - Step 2: `streamText` plus per-sentence checks plus a final whole-text pass, under the flag.
    - Rate and quota in parallel, or one RPC; `getClaims` with the org lookup in parallel; preferences and HCIS inside the existing `Promise.all`.
    - The static prefix first and the clock at the end (§9.13).
    - Intent-based tool subsets and a lower step cap.
  - `finalize.ts`: a per-sentence API.
  - `reply-modality.ts` (§9.13).
- **Migration + rollback:** only if rate and quota are merged into one new DB function (owner-gated); otherwise none.
- **Risk:** high; core answer path and gateway semantics. The flag defaults to off. Step 1's control ④ uses scripted preview fetches (TTFB and total over N runs); Step 2's uses WS-8 numbers.
- **Acceptance test:**
  - A mocked model resolving after 2 s: the Response resolves in under 100 ms and the first SSE chunk is `start`.
  - Preview TTFB and time-to-first-text, p50/p95 over N runs before and after, recorded in the commit.
  - A retract/replace test: a gateway rule that fails late replaces the shown text.
  - Flag off: output byte-identical to today's single delta.
- **Effort:** L.
- **Depends on:**
  - Step 1 (early response): §9.7, §9.2 only; it does not wait for WS-8.
  - Step 2 (sentence streaming behind the flag): WS-8, §9.7, §9.2.
  - Prefix/clock placement and reply mode: §9.13, §9.2.

- **Goal:** users see and can open the sources behind an answer, in chat and in the PDF; the RAG flag opens only on measured numbers.

  - The owner enables the flag in the Lovable env only if both thresholds are met (§9.6).
- **Effort:** L.
- **Depends on:** WS-4, WS-8, §9.4, §9.5, §9.6, §9.3 (e4).

- **IDs:** CHAT-13, CHAT-12, INGEST-36, UX-18, INGEST-02, INGEST-03, INGEST-13, INGEST-14, INGEST-21, INGEST-28, INGEST-31 (structure-aware chunking), INGEST-04, INGEST-05, INGEST-06, INGEST-07, INGEST-08, INGEST-09, INGEST-11, INGEST-12, INGEST-15, INGEST-16, UX-06 (English on SpeechRecognition-only browsers), UX-07, UX-13, UX-14, UX-35, UX-39, UX-40, UX-41, UX-32, UX-09, UI-02, UI-33 (iOS device fixes after the device run), CHAT-27.

## 11. Execution routing (PHASE 15)

**Verified destinations:**

- **(a) Code** — GitHub `faisaljohaimy-ship-it/biz-pulse-ar`, branch `main`, via Claude Code. Gate tsc → eslint → vitest → build → budget → `nexios-qa-guardian`. `git fetch` and read new commits before every push (Article 35). Never force-push, reset or rewrite history.
- **(b) Frontend** — the same repository and gate.
- **(c) Backend / DB** — the production Supabase database managed by Lovable project `6f092c67-70c5-400b-9a7e-8c068475864f` (lead fact, §1.3):
  - SQL only through Lovable MCP `query_database`, at about 8 calls per hour (rate observed by the lead, §1.3).
  - Migrations are idempotent files with a rollback file in `supabase/migrations/`, applied only with owner approval.
  - The Supabase MCP connector is **not** authorized in this session.
- **(d) AI provider** — Gemini through server env in the Lovable deployment (keys not readable here); ElevenLabs server-side for voice.
- **(e) Storage / indexing** — Supabase Storage plus pgvector in the same database.
- **(f) Deployment** — Lovable Publish after the push, then GitHub Actions `production-check.yml` (`workflow_dispatch`); read `latest_commit_sha` to tell "not synced" from "synced but not published". The production domains are blocked from this sandbox.

### Destinations unavailable or blocked

Each line: missing item → owner action → test after access.

- **Supabase MCP connector (not authorized in this session)** → authorize it in the claude.ai connector settings, or keep using Lovable `query_database` → list tables and run one read-only `select` through the connector.
- **DDL through Lovable `query_database` (capability for migrations not verified; about 8 calls per hour, as observed by the lead, §1.3)** → confirm that it accepts DDL, or apply approved migrations from the Lovable/Supabase dashboard → apply and then roll back a no-op idempotent migration on staging.
- **Staging database for migration rehearsal (none)** → provide a staging Supabase project (or a remix with its own database) → apply every WS migration and its rollback; run the preflight under `set local role anon` / `authenticated`.
- **Production network from this sandbox (blocked)** → none possible here; production measurement runs in GitHub Actions or from the owner's browser → `production-check.yml` and `latency-probe.yml` job summaries.
- **CI secret and synthetic tenant for the latency probe** → approve reuse of `E2E_TEST_EMAIL` / `E2E_TEST_PASSWORD` with a synthetic org and user (§9.15) → the probe run writes rows tagged with its `x-aria-request-id` values.
- **Live RLS-isolation credentials (QA-09)** → provide staging credentials (`LIVE` env) → `rls-isolation.test.ts` runs instead of skipping.
- **Production env values (`SADEED_GEMINI_RAG_READY`, `TRUST_PROXY_HEADERS`)** → read them in the Lovable environment settings, or open `GET /api/chat/health` (`rag_ready`) → the values recorded in the WS-4 and WS-7 commit messages.
- **Provider keys (Gemini, ElevenLabs) not readable** → none required for code work (tests use mocks); real-latency numbers come from the deployed environment → latency-probe results.
- **Log drain and alerting destination (QA-17)** → choose Cloudflare Logpush or similar → an alert fires on a synthetic `model_error`.
- **WebKit / iPhone Safari not installed here** → a real-device check on iOS Safari 17/18, in a tab and as a home-screen app → the voice capture, virtual keyboard, PDF download and safe-area checklist (UX-09, UX-32, UI-02, UI-33).
